## 🔵 ANALYTICS SCHEMA — VIEWS

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- ANALYTICS: KEY REPORTING VIEWS
-- ============================================================

-- 1. Daily Hospital Census
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_daily_census AS
SELECT
    a.branch_id,
    b.name AS branch_name,
    DATE(NOW()) AS census_date,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'admitted' THEN 1 END) AS total_inpatients,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'admitted' AND w.ward_type = 'icu' THEN 1 END) AS icu_patients,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'admitted' AND w.ward_type = 'nicu' THEN 1 END) AS nicu_patients,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.admission_datetime::DATE = CURRENT_DATE THEN 1 END) AS new_admissions_today,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN d.discharge_datetime::DATE = CURRENT_DATE THEN 1 END) AS discharges_today,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'admitted' AND w.ward_type = 'maternity' THEN 1 END) AS maternity_patients
FROM clinical.admissions a
JOIN platform.branches b ON a.branch_id = b.id
JOIN facility.wards w ON a.ward_id = w.id
LEFT JOIN clinical.discharges d ON a.id = d.admission_id
GROUP BY a.branch_id, b.name;

-- 2. Bed Occupancy Rate
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_bed_occupancy AS
SELECT
    w.branch_id,
    br.name AS branch_name,
    w.id AS ward_id,
    w.name AS ward_name,
    w.ward_type,
    COUNT(b.id) AS total_beds,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN 1 END) AS occupied_beds,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'available' THEN 1 END) AS available_beds,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'cleaning' THEN 1 END) AS cleaning_beds,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'maintenance' THEN 1 END) AS maintenance_beds,
    ROUND(
        COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
        NULLIF(COUNT(b.id), 0) * 100, 2
    ) AS occupancy_rate_pct
FROM facility.wards w
JOIN platform.branches br ON w.branch_id = br.id
LEFT JOIN facility.beds b ON b.ward_id = w.id AND b.is_active = TRUE
WHERE w.is_active = TRUE
GROUP BY w.branch_id, br.name, w.id, w.name, w.ward_type;

-- 3. Financial Summary View
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_financial_summary AS
SELECT
    b.branch_id,
    br.name AS branch_name,
    DATE_TRUNC('month', b.bill_date) AS month,
    COUNT(b.id) AS total_bills,
    SUM(b.gross_amount) AS total_gross,
    SUM(b.discount_amount) AS total_discount,
    SUM(b.net_amount) AS total_net,
    SUM(b.paid_amount) AS total_collected,
    SUM(b.insurance_amount) AS total_insurance,
    SUM(b.balance_amount) AS total_outstanding,
    SUM(CASE WHEN b.bill_type = 'pharmacy' THEN b.net_amount ELSE 0 END) AS pharmacy_revenue,
    SUM(CASE WHEN b.bill_type = 'opd' THEN b.net_amount ELSE 0 END) AS opd_revenue,
    SUM(CASE WHEN b.bill_type = 'ipd' THEN b.net_amount ELSE 0 END) AS ipd_revenue
FROM finance.bills b
JOIN platform.branches br ON b.branch_id = br.id
WHERE b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')
GROUP BY b.branch_id, br.name, DATE_TRUNC('month', b.bill_date);

-- 4. Doctor Performance View
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_doctor_performance AS
SELECT
    d.id AS doctor_id,
    e.full_name AS doctor_name,
    e.department_id,
    dep.name AS department,
    DATE_TRUNC('month', v.visit_date) AS month,
    COUNT(v.id) AS total_opd_patients,
    COUNT(s.id) AS total_surgeries,
    COUNT(a.id) AS total_admissions,
    ROUND(AVG(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (v.visit_date - v.visit_date)))/60, 0) AS avg_consult_min,
    SUM(bi.net_amount) AS revenue_generated
FROM hr.doctors d
JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
JOIN hr.departments dep ON e.department_id = dep.id
LEFT JOIN clinical.patient_visits v ON v.visit_id IS NOT NULL
LEFT JOIN clinical.surgeries s ON s.primary_surgeon_id = d.id
LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON a.admitting_doctor_id = d.id
LEFT JOIN finance.bill_items bi ON bi.item_ref_id = d.id
GROUP BY d.id, e.full_name, e.department_id, dep.name, DATE_TRUNC('month', v.visit_date);

-- 5. Lab TAT Performance View
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_lab_tat_performance AS
SELECT
    lo.branch_id,
    tm.test_name,
    tc.name AS category,
    COUNT(lr.id) AS total_tests,
    ROUND(AVG(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.entered_at - ls.collected_at))/3600)::NUMERIC, 2) AS avg_tat_hours,
    tm.tat_hours AS target_tat_hours,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.entered_at - ls.collected_at))/3600 > tm.tat_hours THEN 1 END) AS breached_count,
    ROUND(
        COUNT(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.entered_at - ls.collected_at))/3600 > tm.tat_hours THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL 
        / NULLIF(COUNT(lr.id), 0) * 100, 2
    ) AS breach_rate_pct
FROM clinical.lab_results lr
JOIN clinical.lab_order_items loi ON lr.order_item_id = loi.id
JOIN clinical.lab_orders lo ON loi.order_id = lo.id
JOIN clinical.lab_samples ls ON lr.sample_id = ls.id
JOIN clinical.test_master tm ON lr.test_id = tm.id
JOIN clinical.test_categories tc ON tm.category_id = tc.id
WHERE ls.collected_at IS NOT NULL AND lr.entered_at IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY lo.branch_id, tm.test_name, tc.name, tm.tat_hours;

-- 6. Pharmacy Stock Status View
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_pharmacy_stock_status AS
SELECT
    ms.branch_id,
    br.name AS branch_name,
    m.generic_name,
    m.brand_name,
    m.form,
    SUM(ms.quantity) AS total_quantity,
    MIN(ms.expiry_date) AS nearest_expiry,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '30 days' THEN 1 END) AS expiring_30_days,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE THEN 1 END) AS expired_count,
    m.is_controlled,
    m.is_narcotic
FROM pharmacy.medicine_stock ms
JOIN pharmacy.medicines m ON ms.medicine_id = m.id
JOIN platform.branches br ON ms.branch_id = br.id
WHERE ms.status = 'active'
GROUP BY ms.branch_id, br.name, m.id, m.generic_name, m.brand_name, m.form, m.is_controlled, m.is_narcotic;

-- 7. Insurance Claims Aging View  
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_insurance_claims_aging AS
SELECT
    ic.org_id,
    ins.name AS insurance_company,
    COUNT(ic.id) AS total_claims,
    SUM(ic.claimed_amount) AS total_claimed,
    SUM(ic.approved_amount) AS total_approved,
    SUM(ic.settled_amount) AS total_settled,
    SUM(ic.claimed_amount - COALESCE(ic.settled_amount, 0)) AS outstanding,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN ic.status = 'submitted' AND ic.submitted_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days' THEN 1 END) AS aging_30_60,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN ic.status = 'submitted' AND ic.submitted_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '60 days' THEN 1 END) AS aging_60_90,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN ic.status = 'submitted' AND ic.submitted_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '90 days' THEN 1 END) AS aging_90_plus
FROM finance.insurance_claims ic
JOIN finance.insurance_companies ins ON ic.policy_id IS NOT NULL
WHERE ic.status NOT IN ('settled', 'rejected')
GROUP BY ic.org_id, ins.name;
```

---

## 🔵 STORED PROCEDURES — REPORT GENERATORS

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- SP 1: DAILY HOSPITAL CENSUS REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_daily_census_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_date      DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    metric          TEXT,
    value           BIGINT,
    change_vs_prev  BIGINT
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_prev_date DATE := p_date - INTERVAL '1 day';
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    WITH today AS (
        SELECT
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'admitted') AS inpatients,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.admission_datetime::DATE = p_date) AS new_admissions,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE d.discharge_datetime::DATE = p_date) AS discharges,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'admitted' AND w.ward_type = 'icu') AS icu,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'admitted' AND w.ward_type = 'nicu') AS nicu,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'admitted' AND w.ward_type = 'maternity') AS maternity
        FROM clinical.admissions a
        JOIN facility.wards w ON a.ward_id = w.id
        LEFT JOIN clinical.discharges d ON d.admission_id = a.id
        WHERE a.branch_id = p_branch_id
    ),
    prev AS (
        SELECT
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'admitted') AS inpatients,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.admission_datetime::DATE = v_prev_date) AS new_admissions,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE d.discharge_datetime::DATE = v_prev_date) AS discharges
        FROM clinical.admissions a
        LEFT JOIN clinical.discharges d ON d.admission_id = a.id
        WHERE a.branch_id = p_branch_id
    )
    SELECT 'Total Inpatients'::TEXT,    today.inpatients,     today.inpatients - prev.inpatients FROM today, prev UNION ALL
    SELECT 'New Admissions'::TEXT,      today.new_admissions, today.new_admissions - prev.new_admissions FROM today, prev UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Discharges'::TEXT,          today.discharges,     today.discharges - prev.discharges FROM today, prev UNION ALL
    SELECT 'ICU Patients'::TEXT,        today.icu,            0 FROM today, prev UNION ALL
    SELECT 'NICU Patients'::TEXT,       today.nicu,           0 FROM today, prev UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Maternity Patients'::TEXT,  today.maternity,      0 FROM today, prev;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 2: REVENUE REPORT (By Period & Department)
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_revenue_report(
    p_org_id        UUID,
    p_branch_id     UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date     DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date       DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_group_by      TEXT DEFAULT 'day'   -- day/week/month
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    period          TEXT,
    branch_name     TEXT,
    bill_type       TEXT,
    total_bills     BIGINT,
    gross_amount    NUMERIC,
    discount        NUMERIC,
    net_revenue     NUMERIC,
    collected       NUMERIC,
    outstanding     NUMERIC,
    collection_rate NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        CASE p_group_by
            WHEN 'month' THEN TO_CHAR(b.bill_date, 'YYYY-MM')
            WHEN 'week'  THEN TO_CHAR(DATE_TRUNC('week', b.bill_date), 'YYYY-WW')
            ELSE              TO_CHAR(b.bill_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD')
        END                                     AS period,
        br.name                                 AS branch_name,
        COALESCE(b.bill_type, 'All')           AS bill_type,
        COUNT(b.id)::BIGINT                    AS total_bills,
        ROUND(SUM(b.gross_amount)::NUMERIC, 2) AS gross_amount,
        ROUND(SUM(b.discount_amount)::NUMERIC, 2) AS discount,
        ROUND(SUM(b.net_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)   AS net_revenue,
        ROUND(SUM(b.paid_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)  AS collected,
        ROUND(SUM(b.balance_amount)::NUMERIC, 2) AS outstanding,
        ROUND(
            SUM(b.paid_amount) / NULLIF(SUM(b.net_amount), 0) * 100, 2
        )::NUMERIC                             AS collection_rate
    FROM finance.bills b
    JOIN platform.branches br ON b.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE b.org_id = p_org_id
      AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR b.branch_id = p_branch_id)
      AND b.bill_date::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
      AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')
    GROUP BY period, br.name, b.bill_type
    ORDER BY period DESC, br.name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 3: BED OCCUPANCY REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_bed_occupancy_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    ward_name           TEXT,
    ward_type           TEXT,
    total_beds          BIGINT,
    avg_occupied        NUMERIC,
    occupancy_rate_pct  NUMERIC,
    avg_los_days        NUMERIC,
    total_admissions    BIGINT,
    total_discharges    BIGINT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        w.name                                      AS ward_name,
        w.ward_type,
        COUNT(DISTINCT b.id)::BIGINT               AS total_beds,
        ROUND(AVG(
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM clinical.admissions a2
             WHERE a2.ward_id = w.id AND a2.status = 'admitted')
        )::NUMERIC, 1)                             AS avg_occupied,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN b.id END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT b.id), 0) * 100, 2
        )::NUMERIC                                 AS occupancy_rate_pct,
        ROUND(AVG(a.length_of_stay)::NUMERIC, 1)  AS avg_los_days,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN a.admission_datetime::DATE 
                    BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS total_admissions,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN d.discharge_datetime::DATE 
                    BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS total_discharges
    FROM facility.wards w
    JOIN facility.beds b ON b.ward_id = w.id AND b.is_active = TRUE
    LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON a.ward_id = w.id
    LEFT JOIN clinical.discharges d ON d.admission_id = a.id
    WHERE w.branch_id = p_branch_id AND w.is_active = TRUE
    GROUP BY w.name, w.ward_type
    ORDER BY occupancy_rate_pct DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 4: DOCTOR PERFORMANCE REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_doctor_performance_report(
    p_org_id        UUID,
    p_from_date     DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date       DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_department_id UUID DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    doctor_name         TEXT,
    specialization      TEXT,
    department_name     TEXT,
    opd_patients        BIGINT,
    ipd_admissions      BIGINT,
    surgeries           BIGINT,
    revenue_generated   NUMERIC,
    avg_rating          NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        e.full_name                             AS doctor_name,
        d.specialization,
        dep.name                                AS department_name,
        COUNT(DISTINCT v.id)::BIGINT           AS opd_patients,
        COUNT(DISTINCT a.id)::BIGINT           AS ipd_admissions,
        COUNT(DISTINCT s.id)::BIGINT           AS surgeries,
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(bi.net_amount), 0)::NUMERIC, 2) AS revenue_generated,
        ROUND(AVG(4.2)::NUMERIC, 1)            AS avg_rating  -- placeholder for rating
    FROM hr.doctors d
    JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
    JOIN hr.departments dep ON e.department_id = dep.id
    LEFT JOIN clinical.patient_visits v 
        ON v.visit_date::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a 
        ON a.admitting_doctor_id = d.id
        AND a.admission_datetime::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    LEFT JOIN clinical.surgeries s 
        ON s.primary_surgeon_id = d.id
        AND s.actual_start_time::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    LEFT JOIN finance.bill_items bi ON bi.item_ref_id = d.id
    WHERE e.org_id = p_org_id
      AND (p_department_id IS NULL OR e.department_id = p_department_id)
    GROUP BY e.full_name, d.specialization, dep.name
    ORDER BY revenue_generated DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 5: PHARMACY STOCK EXPIRY REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_expiry_report(
    p_branch_id     UUID,
    p_days_ahead    INTEGER DEFAULT 90
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    medicine_name   TEXT,
    brand_name      TEXT,
    batch_no        TEXT,
    expiry_date     DATE,
    days_to_expiry  INTEGER,
    quantity        INTEGER,
    expiry_status   TEXT,
    value           NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        m.generic_name          AS medicine_name,
        m.brand_name,
        ms.batch_no,
        ms.expiry_date,
        (ms.expiry_date - CURRENT_DATE)::INTEGER AS days_to_expiry,
        ms.quantity,
        CASE
            WHEN ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE                      THEN '🔴 EXPIRED'
            WHEN ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '30 days' THEN '🟠 < 30 Days'
            WHEN ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '60 days' THEN '🟡 < 60 Days'
            ELSE                                                         '🟢 < 90 Days'
        END                     AS expiry_status,
        ROUND((ms.quantity * ms.purchase_price)::NUMERIC, 2) AS value
    FROM pharmacy.medicine_stock ms
    JOIN pharmacy.medicines m ON ms.medicine_id = m.id
    WHERE ms.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND ms.expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + (p_days_ahead || ' days')::INTERVAL
      AND ms.quantity > 0
      AND ms.status = 'active'
    ORDER BY ms.expiry_date ASC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 6: INSURANCE CLAIM STATUS REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_insurance_claim_report(
    p_org_id        UUID,
    p_from_date     DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date       DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_status_filter TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    claim_no            TEXT,
    patient_name        TEXT,
    insurance_company   TEXT,
    admission_date      DATE,
    claimed_amount      NUMERIC,
    approved_amount     NUMERIC,
    settled_amount      NUMERIC,
    outstanding         NUMERIC,
    claim_status        TEXT,
    days_pending        INTEGER
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        ic.claim_no,
        p.full_name                             AS patient_name,
        ins.name                                AS insurance_company,
        a.admission_datetime::DATE              AS admission_date,
        ROUND(ic.claimed_amount::NUMERIC, 2)    AS claimed_amount,
        ROUND(COALESCE(ic.approved_amount, 0)::NUMERIC, 2) AS approved_amount,
        ROUND(COALESCE(ic.settled_amount, 0)::NUMERIC, 2)  AS settled_amount,
        ROUND((ic.claimed_amount - COALESCE(ic.settled_amount, 0))::NUMERIC, 2) AS outstanding,
        ic.status                               AS claim_status,
        (CURRENT_DATE - ic.submitted_at::DATE)::INTEGER AS days_pending
    FROM finance.insurance_claims ic
    JOIN clinical.patients p ON ic.patient_id = p.id
    JOIN finance.patient_insurance_policies pip ON ic.policy_id = pip.id
    JOIN finance.insurance_companies ins ON pip.company_id = ins.id
    LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON ic.admission_id = a.id
    WHERE ic.org_id = p_org_id
      AND ic.claim_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
      AND (p_status_filter IS NULL OR ic.status = p_status_filter)
    ORDER BY ic.claim_date DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 7: PATIENT DISCHARGE SUMMARY
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_discharge_summary(
    p_admission_id UUID
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    field   TEXT,
    value   TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT * FROM (
        SELECT 1 AS sort, 'Patient Name'::TEXT,    p.full_name::TEXT
        FROM clinical.admissions a
        JOIN clinical.patients p ON a.patient_id = p.id
        WHERE a.id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 2, 'MRN',            p.mrn::TEXT
        FROM clinical.admissions a JOIN clinical.patients p ON a.patient_id = p.id WHERE a.id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 3, 'Admission No',   a.admission_no::TEXT
        FROM clinical.admissions a WHERE a.id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 4, 'Admission Date', a.admission_datetime::DATE::TEXT
        FROM clinical.admissions a WHERE a.id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 5, 'Discharge Date', d.discharge_datetime::DATE::TEXT
        FROM clinical.discharges d WHERE d.admission_id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 6, 'Length of Stay', a.length_of_stay::TEXT || ' days'
        FROM clinical.admissions a WHERE a.id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 7, 'Discharge Type', d.discharge_type::TEXT
        FROM clinical.discharges d WHERE d.admission_id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 8, 'Final Diagnosis', d.final_diagnosis::TEXT
        FROM clinical.discharges d WHERE d.admission_id = p_admission_id
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 9, 'Total Bill', ROUND(b.net_amount, 2)::TEXT
        FROM finance.bills b WHERE b.admission_id = p_admission_id AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled')
        ORDER BY sort
    ) sub(sort, field, value);
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 8: PAYROLL PROCESSING
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION hr.sp_process_payroll(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_month     INTEGER,
    p_year      INTEGER,
    p_processed_by UUID
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    employee_code   TEXT,
    employee_name   TEXT,
    present_days    NUMERIC,
    gross_salary    NUMERIC,
    deductions      NUMERIC,
    net_salary      NUMERIC,
    status          TEXT
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_run_id UUID;
    v_working_days INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Calculate working days in month
    v_working_days := 26; -- configurable
    
    -- Create payroll run
    INSERT INTO hr.payroll_runs(
        org_id, branch_id, pay_month, pay_year, status, processed_at
    )
    VALUES (p_org_id, p_branch_id, p_month, p_year, 'processing', NOW())
    ON CONFLICT (org_id, branch_id, pay_month, pay_year)
    DO UPDATE SET status = 'processing', processed_at = NOW()
    RETURNING id INTO v_run_id;
    
    -- Process each employee
    INSERT INTO hr.payslips (
        payroll_run_id, employee_id, pay_month, pay_year,
        working_days, present_days, basic_salary,
        gross_salary, total_deduction, net_salary, status
    )
    SELECT
        v_run_id,
        e.id,
        p_month,
        p_year,
        v_working_days,
        COUNT(att.id),
        e.basic_salary,
        e.basic_salary * 1.5,                   -- simplified: basic + allowances
        e.basic_salary * 0.15,                  -- simplified: tax + PF
        e.basic_salary * 1.5 - e.basic_salary * 0.15,
        'draft'
    FROM hr.employees e
    LEFT JOIN hr.attendance att ON att.employee_id = e.id
        AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM att.attendance_date) = p_month
        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM att.attendance_date) = p_year
        AND att.status = 'present'
    WHERE e.org_id = p_org_id
      AND e.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND e.status = 'active'
    GROUP BY e.id, e.basic_salary
    ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
    
    -- Update run status
    UPDATE hr.payroll_runs
    SET status = 'approved',
        total_gross = (SELECT SUM(gross_salary) FROM hr.payslips WHERE payroll_run_id = v_run_id),
        total_deduction = (SELECT SUM(total_deduction) FROM hr.payslips WHERE payroll_run_id = v_run_id),
        total_net = (SELECT SUM(net_salary) FROM hr.payslips WHERE payroll_run_id = v_run_id)
    WHERE id = v_run_id;
    
    -- Return result
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        e.employee_code,
        e.full_name,
        ps.present_days,
        ROUND(ps.gross_salary::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(ps.total_deduction::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(ps.net_salary::NUMERIC, 2),
        ps.status
    FROM hr.payslips ps
    JOIN hr.employees e ON ps.employee_id = e.id
    WHERE ps.payroll_run_id = v_run_id
    ORDER BY e.full_name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 9: OT UTILIZATION REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_ot_utilization_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    ot_room_name        TEXT,
    ot_type             TEXT,
    total_surgeries     BIGINT,
    completed           BIGINT,
    cancelled           BIGINT,
    avg_duration_min    NUMERIC,
    total_ot_hours      NUMERIC,
    cancellation_rate   NUMERIC,
    top_surgeon         TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        ot.name                                 AS ot_room_name,
        ot.ot_type,
        COUNT(s.id)::BIGINT                    AS total_surgeries,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN s.status = 'completed' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS completed,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN s.status = 'cancelled' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS cancelled,
        ROUND(AVG(s.duration_minutes)::NUMERIC, 0) AS avg_duration_min,
        ROUND(SUM(s.duration_minutes)::NUMERIC / 60, 1) AS total_ot_hours,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN s.status = 'cancelled' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(s.id), 0) * 100, 2
        )::NUMERIC AS cancellation_rate,
        (SELECT e2.full_name FROM hr.doctors d2
         JOIN hr.employees e2 ON d2.employee_id = e2.id
         WHERE d2.id = MODE() WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY s.primary_surgeon_id)
         LIMIT 1) AS top_surgeon
    FROM clinical.ot_rooms ot
    LEFT JOIN clinical.surgeries s ON s.ot_room_id = ot.id
        AND s.scheduled_datetime::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    WHERE ot.branch_id = p_branch_id AND ot.is_active = TRUE
    GROUP BY ot.name, ot.ot_type
    ORDER BY total_ot_hours DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 10: EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD KPIs
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_executive_dashboard(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_date      DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    kpi_category    TEXT,
    kpi_name        TEXT,
    current_value   NUMERIC,
    prev_month_value NUMERIC,
    target_value    NUMERIC,
    unit            TEXT,
    status          TEXT     -- green/yellow/red
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    
    -- Clinical KPIs
    SELECT 'Clinical'::TEXT, 'Bed Occupancy Rate'::TEXT,
        (SELECT ROUND(COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
                NULLIF(COUNT(b.id), 0) * 100, 1)
         FROM facility.beds b
         JOIN facility.wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
         JOIN platform.branches br ON w.branch_id = br.id
         WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id AND b.is_active = TRUE),
        85.0::NUMERIC,   -- prev month placeholder
        85.0::NUMERIC,   -- target
        '%'::TEXT,
        'green'::TEXT
    
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Clinical'::TEXT, 'Average Length of Stay'::TEXT,
        (SELECT ROUND(AVG(a.length_of_stay)::NUMERIC, 1)
         FROM clinical.admissions a
         JOIN platform.branches br ON a.branch_id = br.id
         WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
           AND a.admission_datetime >= DATE_TRUNC('month', p_date)),
        4.2::NUMERIC, 4.0::NUMERIC, 'Days'::TEXT, 'green'::TEXT
    
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Financial'::TEXT, 'Monthly Revenue'::TEXT,
        (SELECT ROUND(SUM(b.net_amount)::NUMERIC, 0)
         FROM finance.bills b
         WHERE b.org_id = p_org_id
           AND b.bill_date >= DATE_TRUNC('month', p_date)
           AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')),
        NULL::NUMERIC, NULL::NUMERIC, 'BDT'::TEXT, 'green'::TEXT
    
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Financial'::TEXT, 'Collection Rate'::TEXT,
        (SELECT ROUND(SUM(b.paid_amount) / NULLIF(SUM(b.net_amount), 0) * 100, 1)::NUMERIC
         FROM finance.bills b
         WHERE b.org_id = p_org_id
           AND b.bill_date >= DATE_TRUNC('month', p_date)
           AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')),
        NULL::NUMERIC, 90.0::NUMERIC, '%'::TEXT, 'yellow'::TEXT
    
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Pharmacy'::TEXT, 'Expired Medicine Count'::TEXT,
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC
         FROM pharmacy.medicine_stock ms
         JOIN pharmacy.medicines m ON ms.medicine_id = m.id
         JOIN platform.branches br ON ms.branch_id = br.id
         WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id AND ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE AND ms.quantity > 0),
        NULL::NUMERIC, 0::NUMERIC, 'Batches'::TEXT, 'red'::TEXT;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

## 📊 COMPLETE INDEX STRATEGY

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- PERFORMANCE INDEXES
-- ============================================================

-- Patients
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_org       ON clinical.patients(org_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_mrn       ON clinical.patients(mrn);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_phone     ON clinical.patients(phone);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_nid       ON clinical.patients(nid_no);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_name      ON clinical.patients USING GIN(
    to_tsvector('simple', COALESCE(full_name, '') || ' ' || COALESCE(full_name_bn, ''))
);

-- Admissions
CREATE INDEX idx_admissions_patient ON clinical.admissions(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_admissions_branch  ON clinical.admissions(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_admissions_status  ON clinical.admissions(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_admissions_date    ON clinical.admissions(admission_datetime);

-- Bills
CREATE INDEX idx_bills_patient      ON finance.bills(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_bills_branch       ON finance.bills(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_bills_date         ON finance.bills(bill_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_bills_status       ON finance.bills(status);

-- Lab Results
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_patient ON clinical.lab_results(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_critical ON clinical.lab_results(is_critical) WHERE is_critical = TRUE;

-- Pharmacy Stock
CREATE INDEX idx_stock_branch       ON pharmacy.medicine_stock(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_stock_expiry       ON pharmacy.medicine_stock(expiry_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_stock_medicine     ON pharmacy.medicine_stock(medicine_id);

-- Audit Logs (Partitioned)
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_user         ON audit.audit_logs(user_id, created_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_table        ON audit.audit_logs(table_name, created_at);

-- Attendance
CREATE INDEX idx_attendance_emp     ON hr.attendance(employee_id, attendance_date);
```

## 📋 MODULE → SCHEMA RELATIONSHIP MAP

text

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                   SCHEMA DEPENDENCY MAP                   │
├─────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ platform    │ Core: Orgs → Branches → Users → Roles     │
│             │ All other schemas depend on platform        │
├─────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ clinical    │ Depends: platform, facility, hr            │
│             │ patient → visit → admission → discharge    │
│             │ vitals, notes, diagnoses, OT, nursing      │
├─────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ facility    │ Depends: platform                          │
│             │ building → floor → ward → room → bed       │
├─────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ pharmacy    │ Depends: platform, clinical, finance        │
│             │ medicine → stock → prescription → dispense │
├─────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ finance     │ Depends: platform, clinical                 │
│             │ bill → bill_items → payment → insurance    │
├─────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ hr          │ Depends: platform                          │
│             │ employee → doctor → attendance → payroll   │
├─────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ operations  │ Depends: platform, clinical, facility, hr  │
│             │ housekeeping, diet, kitchen                 │
├─────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ audit       │ Depends: platform                          │
│             │ audit_logs (partitioned), notifications    │
├─────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ analytics   │ Depends: ALL schemas (Views + SPs)         │
│             │ 10 SPs + 7 Views for reporting             │
└─────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

# Hospital ERP - Complete PostgreSQL Schema (Part 4)

## 🔵 DOCTOR TEMPLATE SYSTEM — COMPLETE

SQL

``` sql
-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: DOCTOR TEMPLATE SYSTEM (Complete)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.template_categories (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    description     TEXT,
    specialization  VARCHAR(200),
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    sort_order      INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.doctor_templates (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    doctor_id           UUID REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    category_id         UUID REFERENCES clinical.template_categories(id),
    template_name       VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    template_type       VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
                        -- prescription/note/soap/examination/procedure/
                        -- discharge_summary/referral/certificate/order_set
    specialization      VARCHAR(200),
    
    -- Template Content
    chief_complaint     TEXT,
    history             TEXT,
    examination         TEXT,
    diagnosis           TEXT,
    icd10_codes         JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    
    -- Prescription Items
    medications         JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    -- [{drug_name, generic_name, dose, route, frequency, duration, instructions}]
    
    -- Lab/Radiology Orders
    lab_tests           JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    -- [{test_id, test_name, priority}]
    
    radiology_tests     JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    
    -- Advice/Instructions
    advice              TEXT,
    follow_up_days      INTEGER,
    
    -- Sharing
    is_personal         BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,  -- personal or shared with org
    is_shared           BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    shared_with_roles   JSONB DEFAULT '[]',    -- ['doctor', 'senior_doctor']
    
    -- Usage tracking
    use_count           INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    last_used_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Status
    is_active           BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    is_favourite        BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    sort_order          INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    created_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Update use count trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.update_template_usage()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE clinical.doctor_templates
    SET use_count = use_count + 1,
        last_used_at = NOW()
    WHERE id = NEW.template_id;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TABLE clinical.template_usage_log (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    template_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.doctor_templates(id),
    used_by         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    patient_id      UUID REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    visit_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id),
    admission_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    used_at         TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    modified        BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE  -- was template modified on use
);

CREATE TRIGGER trg_template_usage
AFTER INSERT ON clinical.template_usage_log
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.update_template_usage();

-- Template for order sets (bundle of orders)
CREATE TABLE clinical.order_sets (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name            VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    description     TEXT,
    specialization  VARCHAR(200),
    condition_name  VARCHAR(300),    -- e.g. "Acute MI Protocol", "Sepsis Bundle"
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_by      UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.order_set_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    order_set_id    UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.order_sets(id),
    item_type       VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,   -- medication/lab/radiology/diet/nursing
    item_name       VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    item_detail     JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
    is_mandatory    BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    sort_order      INTEGER DEFAULT 0
);

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: PATIENT DOCUMENTS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.patient_documents (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    document_type   VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
                    -- lab_report/radiology/prescription/consent/
                    -- insurance_card/nid/passport/outside_record/other
    document_name   VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    file_url        TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_size_kb    INTEGER,
    file_type       VARCHAR(20),    -- pdf/jpg/png/dicom
    document_date   DATE,
    source          VARCHAR(100),   -- hospital name / lab name
    notes           TEXT,
    uploaded_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    is_verified     BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    verified_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: RADIOLOGY MODULE (DICOM/PACS)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.radiology_orders (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    patient_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    visit_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id),
    order_no        VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE,
    ordered_by      UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    ordered_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    priority        VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'routine',
    modality        VARCHAR(30),    -- xray/ct/mri/usg/mammography/echo/dexa
    body_part       VARCHAR(100),
    clinical_info   TEXT,
    contrast_used   BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    pregnancy_status VARCHAR(20),
    status          VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'ordered'
                    -- ordered/scheduled/in_progress/completed/reported
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.radiology_order_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    order_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.radiology_orders(id),
    test_id         UUID REFERENCES clinical.test_master(id),
    test_name       VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    price           DECIMAL(12,2),
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending'
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.radiology_reports (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    order_id            UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.radiology_orders(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    modality            VARCHAR(30),
    body_part           VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Findings
    technique           TEXT,
    findings            TEXT,
    impression          TEXT,
    recommendation      TEXT,
    
    -- DICOM
    dicom_study_uid     VARCHAR(100),
    dicom_series_uid    VARCHAR(100),
    image_count         INTEGER,
    image_urls          JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    pacs_url            TEXT,
    
    -- Radiologist
    reported_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    reported_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    verified_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    verified_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Status
    is_critical         BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    critical_notes      TEXT,
    status              VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft',    -- draft/final/amended
    pdf_url             TEXT,
    
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: MICROBIOLOGY MODULE
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.microbiology_reports (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    order_item_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.lab_order_items(id),
    sample_id           UUID REFERENCES clinical.lab_samples(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    
    -- Specimen info
    specimen_type       VARCHAR(100),
    specimen_site       VARCHAR(100),
    collection_method   VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Gram Stain
    gram_stain_result   TEXT,
    
    -- Culture
    organism_1          VARCHAR(200),
    organism_2          VARCHAR(200),
    colony_count        VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Sensitivity (antibiogram)
    sensitivity_data    JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
    -- {ampicillin: S, amoxicillin: R, ciprofloxacin: I, ...}
    
    -- Incubation
    incubation_started  TIMESTAMPTZ,
    incubation_hours    INTEGER DEFAULT 24,
    preliminary_result  TEXT,
    final_result        TEXT,
    
    -- Status
    reported_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    reported_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    verified_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    verified_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    status              VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'incubating',
    
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: HISTOPATHOLOGY MODULE
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.histopathology_reports (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    order_item_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.lab_order_items(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    
    -- Specimen
    specimen_type       VARCHAR(200),
    specimen_site       VARCHAR(200),
    clinical_info       TEXT,
    
    -- Gross description
    gross_description   TEXT,
    
    -- Microscopy
    microscopy          TEXT,
    
    -- IHC
    ihc_markers         JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    -- [{marker, result, interpretation}]
    
    -- Diagnosis
    diagnosis           TEXT,
    snomed_code         VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Pathologist
    reported_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    reported_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    verified_by         UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    verified_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    status              VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'processing',
    
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

## 🔵 DEATH REGISTRATION & MEDICO-LEGAL

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: DEATH REGISTRATION
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.death_registrations (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    
    -- Death details
    death_datetime      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    death_location      VARCHAR(50),    -- ward/icu/er/ot/on_arrival
    ward_id             UUID REFERENCES facility.wards(id),
    bed_id              UUID REFERENCES facility.beds(id),
    
    -- Cause of death
    immediate_cause     VARCHAR(500),   -- Ia
    underlying_cause_1  VARCHAR(500),   -- Ib
    underlying_cause_2  VARCHAR(500),   -- Ic
    contributing_cause  VARCHAR(500),   -- II
    icd10_code          VARCHAR(20) REFERENCES clinical.icd10_codes(code),
    
    -- Mode
    manner_of_death     VARCHAR(50),    -- natural/accident/suicide/homicide/undetermined
    autopsy_required    BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    autopsy_done        BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    autopsy_findings    TEXT,
    
    -- Certificate
    certificate_no      VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE,
    certificate_issued  BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    certificate_date    DATE,
    certificate_url     TEXT,
    
    -- MLC
    is_mlc              BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    mlc_no              VARCHAR(50),
    police_informed     BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    police_station      VARCHAR(200),
    
    -- Attending doctor
    declared_by         UUID REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    declared_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Body
    body_released_to    VARCHAR(200),
    body_released_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    relation            VARCHAR(50),
    
    notes               TEXT,
    created_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto-update patient deceased status
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.mark_patient_deceased()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE clinical.patients
    SET is_deceased = TRUE,
        death_datetime = NEW.death_datetime
    WHERE id = NEW.patient_id;
    
    -- Auto-update admission status
    IF NEW.admission_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE clinical.admissions
        SET status = 'deceased',
            discharge_datetime = NEW.death_datetime
        WHERE id = NEW.admission_id;
        
        -- Free the bed
        UPDATE facility.beds
        SET status = 'cleaning',
            current_patient_id = NULL,
            current_admission_id = NULL
        WHERE current_admission_id = NEW.admission_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_death_registration
AFTER INSERT ON clinical.death_registrations
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.mark_patient_deceased();

-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: MEDICO-LEGAL CASE (MLC)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.mlc_cases (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    visit_id            UUID REFERENCES clinical.patient_visits(id),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    
    mlc_no              VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    registered_at       TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    -- Case details
    case_type           VARCHAR(100),
                        -- rta/assault/poisoning/burn/sexual_assault/
                        -- suspicious_injury/drowning/other
    incident_datetime   TIMESTAMPTZ,
    incident_location   TEXT,
    brought_by          VARCHAR(200),
    relation_to_patient VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Police
    police_station      VARCHAR(200),
    police_officer      VARCHAR(200),
    gd_no               VARCHAR(100),   -- General Diary Number
    police_informed_at  TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Examination findings
    injury_description  TEXT,
    injury_type         JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    -- [{location, type: contusion/laceration/fracture, size, fresh_old}]
    consciousness_level VARCHAR(50),
    alcohol_suspected   BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Legal
    fir_no              VARCHAR(100),   -- First Information Report
    court_order_no      VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Documents
    xray_done           BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    blood_sample_taken  BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    urine_sample_taken  BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    examined_by         UUID REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    examined_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    status              VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'open',
    notes               TEXT,
    created_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

## 🔵 PANDEMIC/OUTBREAK TRACKING

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: DISEASE OUTBREAK TRACKING
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.notifiable_diseases (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name        VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    icd10_code  VARCHAR(20),
    category    VARCHAR(50),    -- epidemic/endemic/pandemic
    is_notifiable BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    report_within_hours INTEGER DEFAULT 24,
    is_active   BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.outbreak_alerts (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    branch_id           UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    disease_id          UUID REFERENCES clinical.notifiable_diseases(id),
    alert_name          VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    start_date          DATE NOT NULL,
    end_date            DATE,
    total_cases         INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    total_deaths        INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    alert_level         VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'watch',
                        -- watch/warning/emergency
    status              VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'active',
    notes               TEXT,
    created_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.outbreak_cases (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    alert_id            UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.outbreak_alerts(id),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    case_date           DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    case_status         VARCHAR(30),    -- suspected/probable/confirmed/negative
    lab_confirmed       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    outcome             VARCHAR(20),    -- recovered/deceased/under_treatment
    notes               TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto-update outbreak case count trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.update_outbreak_count()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE clinical.outbreak_alerts
    SET total_cases = (
        SELECT COUNT(*) FROM clinical.outbreak_cases
        WHERE alert_id = NEW.alert_id
          AND case_status IN ('probable', 'confirmed')
    ),
    total_deaths = (
        SELECT COUNT(*) FROM clinical.outbreak_cases
        WHERE alert_id = NEW.alert_id AND outcome = 'deceased'
    )
    WHERE id = NEW.alert_id;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_outbreak_count
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON clinical.outbreak_cases
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clinical.update_outbreak_count();
```

## 🔵 ADVANCED STORED PROCEDURES (Final Set)

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- SP 26: DOCTOR TEMPLATE USAGE REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_template_usage_report(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    template_name       TEXT,
    template_type       TEXT,
    doctor_name         TEXT,
    specialization      TEXT,
    use_count           BIGINT,
    unique_patients     BIGINT,
    last_used           TIMESTAMPTZ,
    is_shared           BOOLEAN
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        dt.template_name,
        dt.template_type,
        e.full_name                                         AS doctor_name,
        d.specialization,
        COUNT(tul.id)::BIGINT                              AS use_count,
        COUNT(DISTINCT tul.patient_id)::BIGINT             AS unique_patients,
        MAX(tul.used_at)                                    AS last_used,
        dt.is_shared
    FROM clinical.doctor_templates dt
    LEFT JOIN clinical.template_usage_log tul ON tul.template_id = dt.id
        AND tul.used_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    LEFT JOIN hr.doctors d ON dt.doctor_id = d.id
    LEFT JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
    WHERE dt.org_id = p_org_id AND dt.is_active = TRUE
    GROUP BY dt.template_name, dt.template_type, e.full_name,
             d.specialization, dt.is_shared
    ORDER BY use_count DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 27: RADIOLOGY REPORT GENERATION
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_radiology_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    modality            TEXT,
    total_orders        BIGINT,
    completed           BIGINT,
    pending             BIGINT,
    critical_findings   BIGINT,
    avg_report_time_hrs NUMERIC,
    revenue             NUMERIC,
    top_referring_doc   TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        ro.modality,
        COUNT(ro.id)::BIGINT                               AS total_orders,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN ro.status = 'reported' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS completed,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN ro.status NOT IN ('completed', 'reported') THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS pending,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN rr.is_critical = TRUE THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS critical_findings,
        ROUND(AVG(
            EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (rr.reported_at - ro.ordered_at)) / 3600
        )::NUMERIC, 1)                                     AS avg_report_time_hrs,
        ROUND(SUM(COALESCE(roi.price, 0))::NUMERIC, 2)    AS revenue,
        (
            SELECT e2.full_name
            FROM platform.users u2
            JOIN hr.employees e2 ON u2.employee_id = e2.id
            WHERE u2.id = MODE() WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY ro.ordered_by)
            LIMIT 1
        )                                                   AS top_referring_doc
    FROM clinical.radiology_orders ro
    LEFT JOIN clinical.radiology_order_items roi ON roi.order_id = ro.id
    LEFT JOIN clinical.radiology_reports rr ON rr.order_id = ro.id
    WHERE ro.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND ro.ordered_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    GROUP BY ro.modality
    ORDER BY total_orders DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 28: MICROBIOLOGY ANTIBIOGRAM REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_antibiogram_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    organism            TEXT,
    total_isolates      BIGINT,
    antibiotic          TEXT,
    sensitive_pct       NUMERIC,
    intermediate_pct    NUMERIC,
    resistant_pct       NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    WITH expanded AS (
        SELECT
            mr.organism_1 AS organism,
            mr.patient_id,
            key AS antibiotic,
            value::TEXT AS sensitivity
        FROM clinical.microbiology_reports mr,
             jsonb_each_text(mr.sensitivity_data)
        WHERE mr.status = 'final'
          AND mr.organism_1 IS NOT NULL
    )
    SELECT
        e.organism,
        COUNT(DISTINCT e.patient_id)::BIGINT AS total_isolates,
        e.antibiotic,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN UPPER(e.sensitivity) = 'S' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(*), 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC AS sensitive_pct,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN UPPER(e.sensitivity) = 'I' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(*), 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC AS intermediate_pct,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN UPPER(e.sensitivity) = 'R' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(*), 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC AS resistant_pct
    FROM expanded e
    GROUP BY e.organism, e.antibiotic
    ORDER BY e.organism, e.antibiotic;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 29: DEATH STATISTICS REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_mortality_report(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    branch_name         TEXT,
    total_deaths        BIGINT,
    male_deaths         BIGINT,
    female_deaths       BIGINT,
    icu_deaths          BIGINT,
    er_deaths           BIGINT,
    doa                 BIGINT,
    mlc_deaths          BIGINT,
    top_cause           TEXT,
    gross_death_rate    NUMERIC,
    net_death_rate      NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        br.name                                             AS branch_name,
        COUNT(dr.id)::BIGINT                               AS total_deaths,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN p.gender = 'male' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS male_deaths,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN p.gender = 'female' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS female_deaths,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN dr.death_location = 'icu' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS icu_deaths,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN dr.death_location = 'er' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS er_deaths,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN dr.death_location = 'on_arrival' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS doa,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN dr.is_mlc = TRUE THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS mlc_deaths,
        -- Most common cause
        MODE() WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY dr.immediate_cause)  AS top_cause,
        -- Gross death rate = deaths / admissions * 100
        ROUND(
            COUNT(dr.id)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF((
                SELECT COUNT(*) FROM clinical.admissions a2
                WHERE a2.branch_id = br.id
                  AND a2.admission_datetime::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
            ), 0) * 100, 2
        )::NUMERIC                                          AS gross_death_rate,
        -- Net death rate = deaths after 48h / admissions * 100
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN dr.death_datetime > (a.admission_datetime + INTERVAL '48 hours')
                  THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF((
                SELECT COUNT(*) FROM clinical.admissions a3
                WHERE a3.branch_id = br.id
                  AND a3.admission_datetime::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
            ), 0) * 100, 2
        )::NUMERIC                                          AS net_death_rate
    FROM clinical.death_registrations dr
    JOIN clinical.patients p ON dr.patient_id = p.id
    JOIN platform.branches br ON dr.branch_id = br.id
    LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON dr.admission_id = a.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND dr.death_datetime::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    GROUP BY br.name, br.id
    ORDER BY total_deaths DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 30: PATIENT SATISFACTION TREND REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_satisfaction_trend(
    p_org_id        UUID,
    p_months_back   INTEGER DEFAULT 6
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    month               TEXT,
    branch_name         TEXT,
    total_surveys       BIGINT,
    response_rate       NUMERIC,
    avg_rating          NUMERIC,
    nps_score           NUMERIC,
    doctor_rating       NUMERIC,
    nurse_rating        NUMERIC,
    cleanliness_rating  NUMERIC,
    food_rating         NUMERIC,
    billing_rating      NUMERIC,
    complaints_count    BIGINT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        TO_CHAR(DATE_TRUNC('month', pf.submitted_at), 'YYYY-MM') AS month,
        br.name                                                    AS branch_name,
        COUNT(pf.id)::BIGINT                                      AS total_surveys,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(pf.id)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF((
                SELECT COUNT(*) FROM platform.patient_satisfaction_surveys pss
                WHERE pss.org_id = p_org_id
                  AND DATE_TRUNC('month', pss.sent_at) =
                      DATE_TRUNC('month', pf.submitted_at)
            ), 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                                 AS response_rate,
        ROUND(AVG(pf.overall_rating)::NUMERIC, 2)                 AS avg_rating,
        ROUND(
            (COUNT(CASE WHEN pf.nps_score >= 9 THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL -
             COUNT(CASE WHEN pf.nps_score <= 6 THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL) /
            NULLIF(COUNT(pf.id), 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                                 AS nps_score,
        ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'doctor'
               THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2)             AS doctor_rating,
        ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'nurse'
               THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2)             AS nurse_rating,
        ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'cleanliness'
               THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2)             AS cleanliness_rating,
        ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'food'
               THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2)             AS food_rating,
        ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN fq.category = 'billing'
               THEN fr.rating_value END)::NUMERIC, 2)             AS billing_rating,
        COALESCE((
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM operations.complaints c
            WHERE c.org_id = p_org_id
              AND DATE_TRUNC('month', c.created_at) =
                  DATE_TRUNC('month', pf.submitted_at)
        ), 0)::BIGINT                                              AS complaints_count
    FROM operations.patient_feedback pf
    JOIN platform.branches br ON pf.branch_id = br.id
    LEFT JOIN operations.feedback_responses fr ON fr.feedback_id = pf.id
    LEFT JOIN operations.feedback_questions fq ON fr.question_id = fq.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND pf.submitted_at >= NOW() - (p_months_back || ' months')::INTERVAL
    GROUP BY DATE_TRUNC('month', pf.submitted_at), br.name
    ORDER BY month DESC, br.name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 31: INSURANCE PRE-AUTH TRACKING
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_preauth_tracking(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    insurance_company   TEXT,
    total_requests      BIGINT,
    approved            BIGINT,
    pending             BIGINT,
    rejected            BIGINT,
    avg_approval_hours  NUMERIC,
    approval_rate       NUMERIC,
    total_auth_amount   NUMERIC,
    avg_auth_amount     NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        ins.name                                            AS insurance_company,
        COUNT(ic.id)::BIGINT                               AS total_requests,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN ic.status IN ('approved', 'partial', 'settled')
              THEN 1 END)::BIGINT                           AS approved,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN ic.status IN ('submitted', 'query')
              THEN 1 END)::BIGINT                           AS pending,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN ic.status = 'rejected' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS rejected,
        ROUND(AVG(
            CASE WHEN ic.status IN ('approved', 'partial')
            THEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (ic.updated_at - ic.submitted_at)) / 3600
            END
        )::NUMERIC, 1)                                     AS avg_approval_hours,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN ic.status IN ('approved', 'partial', 'settled') THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(ic.id), 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                         AS approval_rate,
        ROUND(SUM(COALESCE(ic.approved_amount, 0))::NUMERIC, 2) AS total_auth_amount,
        ROUND(AVG(COALESCE(ic.approved_amount, 0))::NUMERIC, 2) AS avg_auth_amount
    FROM finance.insurance_claims ic
    JOIN finance.patient_insurance_policies pip ON ic.policy_id = pip.id
    JOIN finance.insurance_companies ins ON pip.company_id = ins.id
    WHERE ic.org_id = p_org_id
      AND ic.claim_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    GROUP BY ins.name
    ORDER BY total_requests DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 32: DIET & NUTRITION COMPLIANCE REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_diet_compliance_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    diet_type           TEXT,
    total_patients      BIGINT,
    meals_ordered       BIGINT,
    meals_delivered     BIGINT,
    meals_missed        BIGINT,
    delivery_rate       NUMERIC,
    avg_patient_rating  NUMERIC,
    complaints          BIGINT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        dt.name                                             AS diet_type,
        COUNT(DISTINCT pdp.patient_id)::BIGINT             AS total_patients,
        COUNT(mdl.id)::BIGINT                              AS meals_ordered,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN mdl.is_delivered = TRUE THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS meals_delivered,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN mdl.is_delivered = FALSE
              AND mdl.delivery_date < CURRENT_DATE THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS meals_missed,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN mdl.is_delivered = TRUE THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(mdl.id), 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                          AS delivery_rate,
        ROUND(AVG(mdl.patient_rating)::NUMERIC, 1)        AS avg_patient_rating,
        0::BIGINT                                           AS complaints
    FROM operations.diet_types dt
    JOIN operations.patient_diet_prescriptions pdp ON pdp.diet_type_id = dt.id
        AND pdp.status = 'active'
    LEFT JOIN operations.meal_delivery_log mdl ON mdl.diet_id = pdp.id
        AND mdl.delivery_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    WHERE pdp.admission_id IN (
        SELECT id FROM clinical.admissions
        WHERE branch_id = p_branch_id AND status = 'admitted'
    )
    GROUP BY dt.name
    ORDER BY total_patients DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 33: FULL FINANCIAL P&L STATEMENT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_pnl_statement(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    line_item       TEXT,
    category        TEXT,
    amount          NUMERIC,
    pct_of_revenue  NUMERIC
) AS $$
DECLARE v_total_revenue NUMERIC;
BEGIN
    -- Get total revenue
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(net_amount), 0) INTO v_total_revenue
    FROM finance.bills
    WHERE org_id = p_org_id
      AND bill_date::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
      AND status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft');

    RETURN QUERY

    -- REVENUE LINES
    SELECT 'IPD Revenue'::TEXT, 'Revenue'::TEXT,
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN bill_type = 'ipd' THEN net_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN bill_type = 'ipd' THEN net_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC /
              NULLIF(v_total_revenue, 0) * 100, 1)
    FROM finance.bills
    WHERE org_id = p_org_id AND bill_date::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
      AND status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')

    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'OPD Revenue', 'Revenue',
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN bill_type = 'opd' THEN net_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN bill_type = 'opd' THEN net_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC /
              NULLIF(v_total_revenue, 0) * 100, 1)
    FROM finance.bills
    WHERE org_id = p_org_id AND bill_date::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
      AND status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')

    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Pharmacy Revenue', 'Revenue',
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN bill_type = 'pharmacy' THEN net_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN bill_type = 'pharmacy' THEN net_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC /
              NULLIF(v_total_revenue, 0) * 100, 1)
    FROM finance.bills
    WHERE org_id = p_org_id AND bill_date::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
      AND status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')

    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Total Revenue', 'Revenue',
        ROUND(v_total_revenue::NUMERIC, 2),
        100.0::NUMERIC
    WHERE v_total_revenue IS NOT NULL

    UNION ALL

    -- EXPENSE LINES (from payroll)
    SELECT 'Staff Salaries', 'Expense',
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(net_salary), 0)::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(net_salary), 0)::NUMERIC /
              NULLIF(v_total_revenue, 0) * 100, 1)
    FROM hr.payslips ps
    JOIN hr.payroll_runs pr ON ps.payroll_run_id = pr.id
    WHERE pr.org_id = p_org_id AND pr.status = 'paid'
      AND TO_DATE(pr.pay_year::TEXT || '-' || pr.pay_month::TEXT || '-01', 'YYYY-MM-DD')
          BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date

    UNION ALL

    -- Medicine cost
    SELECT 'Medicine Cost', 'Expense',
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(di.quantity * di.unit_price), 0)::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(di.quantity * di.unit_price), 0)::NUMERIC /
              NULLIF(v_total_revenue, 0) * 100, 1)
    FROM pharmacy.dispensing_items di
    JOIN pharmacy.dispensings d ON di.dispensing_id = d.id
    JOIN platform.branches br ON d.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND d.dispensed_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date

    UNION ALL

    -- Maintenance cost
    SELECT 'Maintenance Cost', 'Expense',
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(total_cost), 0)::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(total_cost), 0)::NUMERIC /
              NULLIF(v_total_revenue, 0) * 100, 1)
    FROM facility.maintenance_work_orders mwo
    JOIN facility.equipment_registry er ON mwo.equipment_id = er.id
    JOIN platform.branches br ON er.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND mwo.completed_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 34: SECURITY AUDIT REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_security_audit(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE - 7,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    audit_category      TEXT,
    metric              TEXT,
    count_value         BIGINT,
    risk_level          TEXT,
    details             TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY

    -- Failed login attempts
    SELECT 'Authentication'::TEXT, 'Failed Login Attempts'::TEXT,
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        CASE WHEN COUNT(*) > 100 THEN '🔴 High'
             WHEN COUNT(*) > 20 THEN '🟡 Medium'
             ELSE '🟢 Low' END,
        'Users: ' || STRING_AGG(DISTINCT username, ', ')
    FROM audit.login_history
    WHERE status = 'failed'
      AND login_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date

    UNION ALL

    -- Locked accounts
    SELECT 'Authentication', 'Locked Accounts',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        CASE WHEN COUNT(*) > 0 THEN '🟡 Review' ELSE '🟢 None' END,
        'Accounts locked due to failed attempts'
    FROM platform.users
    WHERE locked_until > NOW()

    UNION ALL

    -- Permission changes
    SELECT 'Access Control', 'Permission Changes',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        '🟡 Review',
        'Review all permission modifications'
    FROM audit.audit_logs
    WHERE table_name = 'role_permissions'
      AND action IN ('INSERT', 'DELETE')
      AND created_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date

    UNION ALL

    -- Patient data exports
    SELECT 'Data Access', 'Patient Data Exports',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        CASE WHEN COUNT(*) > 50 THEN '🟡 Review' ELSE '🟢 Normal' END,
        'Monitor for unauthorized exports'
    FROM audit.audit_logs
    WHERE table_name = 'patients'
      AND action = 'SELECT'
      AND created_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date

    UNION ALL

    -- Deleted records
    SELECT 'Data Integrity', 'Deleted Records',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        CASE WHEN COUNT(*) > 0 THEN '🔴 Investigate' ELSE '🟢 None' END,
        'Tables: ' || STRING_AGG(DISTINCT table_name, ', ')
    FROM audit.audit_logs
    WHERE action = 'DELETE'
      AND created_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date

    UNION ALL

    -- After-hours access
    SELECT 'Access Pattern', 'After-Hours System Access',
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT,
        CASE WHEN COUNT(*) > 20 THEN '🟡 Review' ELSE '🟢 Normal' END,
        'Access between 10PM - 6AM'
    FROM audit.login_history
    WHERE status = 'success'
      AND EXTRACT(HOUR FROM login_at) NOT BETWEEN 6 AND 22
      AND login_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 35: COMPLETE WARD ROUND REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_ward_round_report(
    p_ward_id   UUID,
    p_date      DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    bed_no              TEXT,
    room_no             TEXT,
    patient_name        TEXT,
    mrn                 TEXT,
    age_gender          TEXT,
    admission_date      DATE,
    los_days            INTEGER,
    primary_diagnosis   TEXT,
    doctor_name         TEXT,
    last_vitals         JSONB,
    active_medications  TEXT,
    pending_labs        BIGINT,
    pending_tasks       TEXT,
    diet_type           TEXT,
    discharge_ready     BOOLEAN,
    alerts              TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        b.bed_no,
        r.room_no,
        p.full_name                                         AS patient_name,
        p.mrn,
        COALESCE(p.age_years::TEXT, EXTRACT(YEAR FROM AGE(p.date_of_birth))::TEXT)
            || 'Y / ' || UPPER(LEFT(p.gender, 1))          AS age_gender,
        a.admission_datetime::DATE                          AS admission_date,
        EXTRACT(DAY FROM NOW() - a.admission_datetime)::INTEGER AS los_days,
        a.primary_diagnosis,
        e.full_name                                         AS doctor_name,
        -- Last vitals as JSON
        (SELECT row_to_json(vs_row) FROM (
            SELECT vs.bp_systolic, vs.bp_diastolic, vs.pulse_rate,
                   vs.temperature, vs.spo2, vs.recorded_at
            FROM clinical.vital_signs vs
            WHERE vs.patient_id = p.id
            ORDER BY vs.recorded_at DESC LIMIT 1
        ) vs_row)::JSONB                                    AS last_vitals,
        -- Active medications summary
        (SELECT STRING_AGG(mo.drug_name, ', ')
         FROM clinical.medication_orders mo
         WHERE mo.patient_id = p.id AND mo.status = 'active'
         LIMIT 5)                                           AS active_medications,
        -- Pending lab count
        (SELECT COUNT(*)
         FROM clinical.lab_orders lo
         WHERE lo.admission_id = a.id
           AND lo.status NOT IN ('resulted', 'verified'))::BIGINT AS pending_labs,
        -- Pending nursing tasks
        'See MAR'::TEXT                                     AS pending_tasks,
        -- Diet type
        (SELECT dt.name FROM operations.patient_diet_prescriptions pdp
         JOIN operations.diet_types dt ON pdp.diet_type_id = dt.id
         WHERE pdp.patient_id = p.id AND pdp.status = 'active'
         ORDER BY pdp.created_at DESC LIMIT 1)              AS diet_type,
        -- Discharge readiness
        (NOT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM clinical.lab_orders lo
            WHERE lo.admission_id = a.id AND lo.status NOT IN ('resulted', 'verified')
        ) AND NOT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM finance.bills bi
            WHERE bi.admission_id = a.id AND bi.balance_amount > 0
        ))                                                  AS discharge_ready,
        -- Alerts
        CASE
            WHEN p.is_mlc THEN '🚨 MLC Case'
            WHEN p.is_vip THEN '⭐ VIP Patient'
            WHEN EXTRACT(DAY FROM NOW() - a.admission_datetime) > 14 THEN '⚠️ Long Stay'
            ELSE ''
        END                                                 AS alerts
    FROM facility.beds b
    LEFT JOIN facility.rooms r ON b.room_id = r.id
    LEFT JOIN clinical.patients p ON b.current_patient_id = p.id
    LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON b.current_admission_id = a.id
    LEFT JOIN hr.doctors d ON a.admitting_doctor_id = d.id
    LEFT JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
    WHERE b.ward_id = p_ward_id
      AND b.status = 'occupied'
      AND b.is_active = TRUE
    ORDER BY r.room_no, b.bed_no;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```
