## 🔵 MATERIALIZED VIEWS FOR PERFORMANCE

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- MATERIALIZED VIEWS: DASHBOARD PERFORMANCE
-- ============================================================

-- 1. Monthly Revenue Summary (Refresh daily)
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW analytics.mv_monthly_revenue AS
SELECT
    b.org_id,
    b.branch_id,
    br.name                                             AS branch_name,
    DATE_TRUNC('month', b.bill_date)                   AS month,
    b.bill_type,
    COUNT(b.id)                                         AS total_bills,
    ROUND(SUM(b.gross_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)             AS gross_revenue,
    ROUND(SUM(b.discount_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)          AS total_discount,
    ROUND(SUM(b.net_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)               AS net_revenue,
    ROUND(SUM(b.paid_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)              AS collected,
    ROUND(SUM(b.insurance_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)         AS insurance_revenue,
    ROUND(SUM(b.balance_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)           AS outstanding
FROM finance.bills b
JOIN platform.branches br ON b.branch_id = br.id
WHERE b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')
GROUP BY b.org_id, b.branch_id, br.name,
         DATE_TRUNC('month', b.bill_date), b.bill_type;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ON analytics.mv_monthly_revenue(org_id, branch_id, month, bill_type);

-- 2. Daily Admission Summary (Refresh hourly)
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW analytics.mv_daily_admissions AS
SELECT
    a.branch_id,
    br.name                                             AS branch_name,
    a.admission_datetime::DATE                         AS admission_date,
    COUNT(a.id)                                         AS total_admissions,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.admission_type = 'emergency' THEN 1 END) AS emergency_admissions,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'admitted' THEN 1 END)   AS currently_admitted,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'discharged' THEN 1 END) AS discharged_count,
    ROUND(AVG(a.length_of_stay)::NUMERIC, 1)           AS avg_los
FROM clinical.admissions a
JOIN platform.branches br ON a.branch_id = br.id
GROUP BY a.branch_id, br.name, a.admission_datetime::DATE;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ON analytics.mv_daily_admissions(branch_id, admission_date);

-- 3. Doctor Performance Monthly (Refresh daily)
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW analytics.mv_doctor_monthly AS
SELECT
    d.id                                                AS doctor_id,
    e.full_name                                         AS doctor_name,
    e.org_id,
    e.department_id,
    dep.name                                            AS department_name,
    DATE_TRUNC('month', a.admission_datetime)          AS month,
    COUNT(DISTINCT a.id)                               AS total_admissions,
    COUNT(DISTINCT s.id)                               AS total_surgeries,
    ROUND(AVG(a.length_of_stay)::NUMERIC, 1)           AS avg_patient_los,
    ROUND(SUM(COALESCE(bi.net_amount, 0))::NUMERIC, 2) AS revenue_generated
FROM hr.doctors d
JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
JOIN hr.departments dep ON e.department_id = dep.id
LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON a.admitting_doctor_id = d.id
LEFT JOIN clinical.surgeries s ON s.primary_surgeon_id = d.id
    AND DATE_TRUNC('month', s.scheduled_datetime) =
        DATE_TRUNC('month', a.admission_datetime)
LEFT JOIN finance.bill_items bi ON bi.item_ref_id = d.id
WHERE a.id IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY d.id, e.full_name, e.org_id, e.department_id,
         dep.name, DATE_TRUNC('month', a.admission_datetime);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ON analytics.mv_doctor_monthly(doctor_id, month);

-- Refresh all materialized views function
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.refresh_all_mv()
RETURNS VOID AS $$
BEGIN
    REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY analytics.mv_monthly_revenue;
    REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY analytics.mv_daily_admissions;
    REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY analytics.mv_doctor_monthly;
    REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY operations.mv_current_stock;
    RAISE NOTICE 'All materialized views refreshed at %', NOW();
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

## 🔵 DATABASE MAINTENANCE & HOUSEKEEPING

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- PARTITION MANAGEMENT: CREATE FUTURE PARTITIONS
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION audit.create_monthly_partition(
    p_year  INTEGER,
    p_month INTEGER
)
RETURNS VOID AS $$
DECLARE
    v_table_name TEXT;
    v_start_date DATE;
    v_end_date   DATE;
BEGIN
    v_table_name := 'audit.audit_logs_' ||
                    LPAD(p_year::TEXT, 4, '0') || '_' ||
                    LPAD(p_month::TEXT, 2, '0');
    v_start_date := TO_DATE(p_year::TEXT || '-' || p_month::TEXT || '-01', 'YYYY-MM-DD');
    v_end_date   := v_start_date + INTERVAL '1 month';

    EXECUTE FORMAT(
        'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS %s PARTITION OF audit.audit_logs
         FOR VALUES FROM (%L) TO (%L)',
        v_table_name, v_start_date, v_end_date
    );

    RAISE NOTICE 'Created partition: % for range % to %',
        v_table_name, v_start_date, v_end_date;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Create partitions for 2025-2026
DO $$
DECLARE v_month INTEGER;
BEGIN
    FOR v_month IN 1..12 LOOP
        PERFORM audit.create_monthly_partition(2025, v_month);
        PERFORM audit.create_monthly_partition(2026, v_month);
    END LOOP;
END $$;

-- ============================================================
-- DATA ARCHIVAL FUNCTION
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE audit.archived_audit_logs (
    LIKE audit.audit_logs INCLUDING ALL
);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION audit.archive_old_logs(p_retention_days INTEGER DEFAULT 365)
RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
DECLARE v_archived INTEGER;
BEGIN
    WITH moved AS (
        DELETE FROM audit.audit_logs
        WHERE created_at < NOW() - (p_retention_days || ' days')::INTERVAL
        RETURNING *
    )
    INSERT INTO audit.archived_audit_logs SELECT * FROM moved;

    GET DIAGNOSTICS v_archived = ROW_COUNT;
    RAISE NOTICE 'Archived % audit log records', v_archived;
    RETURN v_archived;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SOFT DELETE PATTERN (For sensitive tables)
-- ============================================================

-- Add soft delete to patients
ALTER TABLE clinical.patients
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS deleted_by UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS delete_reason TEXT;

-- Soft delete function
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clinical.soft_delete_patient(
    p_patient_id UUID,
    p_deleted_by UUID,
    p_reason     TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE clinical.patients
    SET deleted_at = NOW(),
        deleted_by = p_deleted_by,
        delete_reason = p_reason
    WHERE id = p_patient_id
      AND deleted_at IS NULL;

    IF FOUND THEN
        INSERT INTO audit.audit_logs(
            user_id, action, schema_name, table_name,
            record_id, new_values, created_at
        ) VALUES (
            p_deleted_by, 'SOFT_DELETE', 'clinical', 'patients',
            p_patient_id::TEXT,
            jsonb_build_object('reason', p_reason, 'deleted_at', NOW()),
            NOW()
        );
        RETURN TRUE;
    END IF;
    RETURN FALSE;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Row-level security for soft-deleted patients
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW clinical.active_patients AS
SELECT * FROM clinical.patients WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

-- ============================================================
-- PERFORMANCE MONITORING
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE audit.query_performance_log (
    id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    query_hash      BIGINT,
    query_text      TEXT,
    execution_time  DECIMAL(10,3),  -- milliseconds
    rows_returned   INTEGER,
    user_id         UUID,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Slow query alert (called from application layer)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION audit.log_slow_query(
    p_query         TEXT,
    p_exec_time_ms  DECIMAL,
    p_rows          INTEGER,
    p_user_id       UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_threshold_ms  DECIMAL DEFAULT 1000
)
RETURNS VOID AS $$
BEGIN
    IF p_exec_time_ms > p_threshold_ms THEN
        INSERT INTO audit.query_performance_log(
            query_hash, query_text, execution_time,
            rows_returned, user_id, created_at
        ) VALUES (
            HASHTEXT(p_query), p_query, p_exec_time_ms,
            p_rows, p_user_id, NOW()
        );
    END IF;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

## 📊 FINAL COMPLETE SCHEMA SUMMARY

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- SCHEMA OBJECT COUNT VERIFICATION QUERY
-- ============================================================
SELECT
    schemaname                          AS schema_name,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN object_type = 'table'     THEN 1 END) AS tables,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN object_type = 'view'      THEN 1 END) AS views,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN object_type = 'matview'   THEN 1 END) AS mat_views,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN object_type = 'function'  THEN 1 END) AS functions,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN object_type = 'trigger'   THEN 1 END) AS triggers,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN object_type = 'sequence'  THEN 1 END) AS sequences,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN object_type = 'index'     THEN 1 END) AS indexes
FROM (
    SELECT schemaname, 'table'    AS object_type FROM pg_tables
    UNION ALL
    SELECT schemaname, 'view'     FROM pg_views
    UNION ALL
    SELECT schemaname, 'matview'  FROM pg_matviews
    UNION ALL
    SELECT routine_schema, 'function' FROM information_schema.routines
    UNION ALL
    SELECT trigger_schema, 'trigger'  FROM information_schema.triggers
    UNION ALL
    SELECT schemaname, 'sequence' FROM pg_sequences
    UNION ALL
    SELECT schemaname, 'index'    FROM pg_indexes
) obj
WHERE schemaname IN (
    'platform','clinical','facility','pharmacy',
    'finance','hr','operations','audit','analytics'
)
GROUP BY schemaname
ORDER BY schemaname;
```

```
│  │ complaints → feedback → feedback_responses                    │
│  │                                                               │
│  audit ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────    │
│  │ audit_logs (PARTITIONED) → archived_audit_logs               │
│  │ login_history → notifications_queue → notification_logs      │
│  │ notification_templates → query_performance_log               │
│  │                                                               │
│  analytics ─────────────────────────────────────────────────    │
│  │ 12 Views + 3 Materialized Views + 35 Stored Procedures       │
│  │ Executive Dashboard, Clinical, Financial, Quality Reports     │
│                                                                   │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

TRIGGERS (52): Auto-MRN, Auto-LOS, Auto-BMI, Auto-Bill-Balance,
               Payment-Update, Bed-Transfer, Auto-Cleaning,
               Critical-Lab-Alert, Drug-Dispense-Stock, Low-Stock,
               Audit-Log (Universal), Journal-Balance, Death-Mark,
               Outbreak-Count, Template-Usage, Slot-Count,
               Ambulance-Status, Blood-Issue-Status, Reagent-Usage,
               GRN-Post-Ledger, Indent-Post-Ledger + 30 more

STORED PROCS (35): Daily Census, Revenue Report, Bed Occupancy,
                   Doctor Performance, Lab TAT, Pharmacy Expiry,
                   Insurance Claims, Discharge Summary, Payroll,
                   OT Utilization, Executive Dashboard, Procurement,
                   Stock Consumption, Attendance, Equipment Status,
                   Blood Bank, Complaint Analysis, Trial Balance,
                   Branch Comparison, Patient History, Infection Control,
                   Reagent Consumption, Ambulance Performance,
                   Waste Management, Visitor Log, Quality Indicators,
                   Template Usage, Radiology, Antibiogram, Mortality,
                   Satisfaction Trend, Pre-Auth Tracking, Diet Compliance,
                   P&L Statement, Security Audit, Ward Round, + more
```

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- CANTEEN MANAGEMENT — COMPLETE MODULE

CREATE TABLE operations.canteen_locations (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    location        VARCHAR(200),
    floor_id        UUID REFERENCES facility.floors(id),
    manager_id      UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    seating_capacity INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    operating_hours VARCHAR(100),   -- e.g. "07:00-22:00"
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.canteen_menu_categories (

CREATE TABLE operations.canteen_menu_items (

    category_id     UUID REFERENCES operations.canteen_menu_categories(id),
    item_name       VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    item_name_bn    VARCHAR(200),

    price           DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
    cost_price      DECIMAL(10,2),
    item_type       VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'food',  -- food/beverage/combo
    is_vegetarian   BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,

    photo_url       TEXT,
    is_available    BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    sort_order      INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.canteen_orders (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    canteen_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.canteen_locations(id),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    order_no        VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    order_type      VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'counter',  -- counter/table/online/ward
    
    -- Customer
    customer_type   VARCHAR(20),    -- staff/patient/visitor/external
    customer_id     UUID,           -- employee_id or patient_id
    customer_name   VARCHAR(200),
    table_no        VARCHAR(20),
    ward_delivery   UUID REFERENCES facility.wards(id),
    
    -- Amounts
    subtotal        DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
    discount_amount DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
    tax_amount      DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
    total_amount    DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Payment
    payment_method  VARCHAR(30),    -- cash/card/bkash/nagad/staff_deduction/credit
    paid_amount     DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
    change_amount   DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
    payment_status  VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending',
    
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending',
                    -- pending/preparing/ready/delivered/cancelled
    served_by       UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    notes           TEXT,
    ordered_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    served_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.canteen_order_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    order_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.canteen_orders(id),
    menu_item_id    UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.canteen_menu_items(id),
    item_name       VARCHAR(200),
    quantity        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    unit_price      DECIMAL(10,2),
    total_price     DECIMAL(12,2),
    special_request TEXT
);

-- Auto-compute canteen order total
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.compute_canteen_total()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE operations.canteen_orders co
    SET
        subtotal = (
            SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total_price), 0)
            FROM operations.canteen_order_items
            WHERE order_id = NEW.order_id
        ),
        total_amount = (
            SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total_price), 0)
            FROM operations.canteen_order_items
            WHERE order_id = NEW.order_id
        ) - COALESCE(co.discount_amount, 0) + COALESCE(co.tax_amount, 0)
    WHERE co.id = NEW.order_id;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_canteen_total
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON operations.canteen_order_items
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.compute_canteen_total();

CREATE TABLE operations.canteen_daily_summary (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    canteen_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.canteen_locations(id),
    summary_date    DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    total_orders    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    total_revenue   DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    cash_revenue    DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    card_revenue    DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    mobile_revenue  DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    credit_revenue  DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    total_discount  DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    opening_cash    DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    closing_cash    DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    variance        DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    prepared_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    approved_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    notes           TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    UNIQUE(canteen_id, summary_date)
);

-- Auto-generate canteen order number
CREATE SEQUENCE operations.canteen_order_seq START 100001;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.generate_canteen_order_no()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.order_no IS NULL THEN
        NEW.order_no := 'CAN' || LPAD(nextval('operations.canteen_order_seq')::TEXT, 6, '0');
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_canteen_order_no
BEFORE INSERT ON operations.canteen_orders
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.generate_canteen_order_no();

-- Auto-generate kitchen order from diet prescription
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.sync_kitchen_from_diet()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    v_branch_id UUID;
    v_meal_types RECORD;
BEGIN
    -- Get branch
    SELECT branch_id INTO v_branch_id
    FROM clinical.admissions WHERE id = NEW.admission_id;

    -- Create meal plans for each meal type
    FOR v_meal_types IN
        SELECT id FROM operations.meal_types WHERE is_active = TRUE
    LOOP
        INSERT INTO operations.kitchen_meal_plans(
            branch_id, plan_date, diet_type_id, meal_type_id, planned_count, status
        )
        VALUES (
            v_branch_id, NEW.start_date, NEW.diet_type_id,
            v_meal_types.id, 1, 'planned'
        )
        ON CONFLICT (branch_id, plan_date, diet_type_id, meal_type_id)
        DO UPDATE SET planned_count = operations.kitchen_meal_plans.planned_count + 1;
    END LOOP;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_kitchen_from_diet
AFTER INSERT ON operations.patient_diet_prescriptions
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.sync_kitchen_from_diet();

CREATE TABLE operations.linen_categories (
    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name        VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,   -- Bed Sheet/Pillow Cover/Blanket/Towel/Gown/Curtain
    code        VARCHAR(20),
    wash_cycle  INTEGER DEFAULT 1,      -- expected washes before condemnation
    is_active   BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE TABLE operations.linen_inventory (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    category_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.linen_categories(id),
    total_quantity  INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    in_ward         INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    in_laundry      INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    condemned       INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    available       INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS
                    (total_quantity - in_ward - in_laundry - condemned) STORED,
    last_counted    DATE,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE operations.linen_issuance (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    ward_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES facility.wards(id),
    bed_id          UUID REFERENCES facility.beds(id),
    category_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES operations.linen_categories(id),
    patient_id      UUID REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    quantity        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    issued_by       UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    issued_at       TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    returned_at     TIMESTAMPTZ,
    returned_qty    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'issued'  -- issued/returned/missing/condemned
);

CREATE TABLE operations.laundry_batches (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    batch_no        VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE,
    collection_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    sent_date       DATE,
    returned_date   DATE,
    laundry_vendor  VARCHAR(200),
    items           JSONB DEFAULT '[]',  -- [{category_id, quantity}]
    total_items     INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    returned_items  INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    cost            DECIMAL(12,2),
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'collected',
                    -- collected/sent/washing/returned/verified
    notes           TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Update linen inventory on issuance
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION operations.update_linen_on_issue()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN
        UPDATE operations.linen_inventory
        SET in_ward = in_ward + NEW.quantity,
            updated_at = NOW()
        WHERE branch_id = NEW.branch_id
          AND category_id = NEW.category_id;

    ELSIF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND NEW.status = 'returned' AND OLD.status = 'issued' THEN
        UPDATE operations.linen_inventory
        SET in_ward = GREATEST(in_ward - NEW.returned_qty, 0),
            updated_at = NOW()
        WHERE branch_id = NEW.branch_id
          AND category_id = NEW.category_id;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_linen_inventory
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON operations.linen_issuance
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION operations.update_linen_on_issue();
```

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- FINANCE: CORPORATE BILLING (Complete)
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE finance.corporate_companies (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    company_name        VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    company_code        VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE,
    contact_person      VARCHAR(200),
    phone               VARCHAR(20),
    email               VARCHAR(150),
    address             TEXT,
    bin_no              VARCHAR(50),
    tin_no              VARCHAR(50),
    contract_start      DATE,
    contract_end        DATE,
    credit_limit        DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    credit_days         INTEGER DEFAULT 30,
    discount_pct        DECIMAL(5,2) DEFAULT 0,
    allowed_services    JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    is_active           BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE finance.corporate_employees (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    company_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.corporate_companies(id),
    employee_name       VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    employee_id_no      VARCHAR(50),
    designation         VARCHAR(100),
    phone               VARCHAR(20),
    coverage_limit      DECIMAL(12,2),
    dependent_coverage  BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    valid_from          DATE,
    valid_to            DATE,
    is_active           BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE finance.corporate_invoices (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    company_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.corporate_companies(id),
    invoice_no          VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    invoice_date        DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    period_from         DATE,
    period_to           DATE,
    gross_amount        DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    discount_amount     DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    tax_amount          DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    net_amount          DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    paid_amount         DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    balance_amount      DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    due_date            DATE,
    status              VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft',
                        -- draft/sent/partial/paid/overdue
    notes               TEXT,
    created_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE finance.corporate_invoice_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    invoice_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.corporate_invoices(id),
    bill_id         UUID REFERENCES finance.bills(id),
    patient_name    VARCHAR(200),
    employee_id_no  VARCHAR(50),
    service_date    DATE,
    description     VARCHAR(300),
    amount          DECIMAL(12,2),
    discount        DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
    net_amount      DECIMAL(12,2)
);

-- ============================================================
-- FINANCE: PETTY CASH MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE finance.petty_cash_funds (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    fund_name       VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    custodian_id    UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    opening_balance DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
    current_balance DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
    max_limit       DECIMAL(12,2),
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE finance.petty_cash_transactions (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    fund_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.petty_cash_funds(id),
    transaction_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    transaction_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,   -- expense/replenishment/opening
    description     VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    amount          DECIMAL(12,2) NOT NULL,
    category        VARCHAR(100),   -- office_supplies/transport/misc
    voucher_no      VARCHAR(50),
    approved_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    receipt_url     TEXT,
    balance_after   DECIMAL(12,2),
    created_by      UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Update petty cash balance
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION finance.update_petty_cash_balance()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.transaction_type = 'expense' THEN
        UPDATE finance.petty_cash_funds
        SET current_balance = current_balance - NEW.amount
        WHERE id = NEW.fund_id;
    ELSE
        UPDATE finance.petty_cash_funds
        SET current_balance = current_balance + NEW.amount
        WHERE id = NEW.fund_id;
    END IF;

    SELECT current_balance INTO NEW.balance_after
    FROM finance.petty_cash_funds WHERE id = NEW.fund_id;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_petty_cash
BEFORE INSERT ON finance.petty_cash_transactions
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION finance.update_petty_cash_balance();

-- ============================================================
-- FINANCE: BUDGET MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE finance.budgets (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    branch_id       UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    department_id   UUID REFERENCES hr.departments(id),
    fiscal_year     INTEGER NOT NULL,
    budget_type     VARCHAR(30),    -- capex/opex/revenue
    account_id      UUID REFERENCES finance.chart_of_accounts(id),
    annual_budget   DECIMAL(16,2) NOT NULL,
    q1_budget       DECIMAL(14,2),
    q2_budget       DECIMAL(14,2),
    q3_budget       DECIMAL(14,2),
    q4_budget       DECIMAL(14,2),
    notes           TEXT,
    approved_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft',
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE finance.budget_actuals (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    budget_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES finance.budgets(id),
    period_month    INTEGER NOT NULL,
    period_year     INTEGER NOT NULL,
    actual_amount   DECIMAL(14,2) DEFAULT 0,
    variance        DECIMAL(14,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS
                    (
                        CASE WHEN period_month <= 3 THEN q1_budget/3
                             WHEN period_month <= 6 THEN q2_budget/3
                             WHEN period_month <= 9 THEN q3_budget/3
                             ELSE q4_budget/3
                        END - actual_amount
                    ) STORED,
    notes           TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Budget vs Actual View
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_budget_vs_actual AS
SELECT
    b.org_id,
    b.branch_id,
    br.name                                             AS branch_name,
    dep.name                                            AS department_name,
    b.fiscal_year,
    b.budget_type,
    coa.account_name,
    b.annual_budget,
    COALESCE(SUM(ba.actual_amount), 0)                 AS actual_ytd,
    b.annual_budget - COALESCE(SUM(ba.actual_amount), 0) AS variance,
    ROUND(
        COALESCE(SUM(ba.actual_amount), 0) /
        NULLIF(b.annual_budget, 0) * 100, 1
    )                                                   AS utilization_pct,
    CASE
        WHEN ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(ba.actual_amount), 0) /
                   NULLIF(b.annual_budget, 0) * 100, 1) > 100 THEN '🔴 Over Budget'
        WHEN ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(ba.actual_amount), 0) /
                   NULLIF(b.annual_budget, 0) * 100, 1) > 85  THEN '🟡 Near Limit'
        ELSE '🟢 On Track'
    END                                                 AS budget_status
FROM finance.budgets b
LEFT JOIN finance.budget_actuals ba ON ba.budget_id = b.id
LEFT JOIN platform.branches br ON b.branch_id = br.id
LEFT JOIN hr.departments dep ON b.department_id = dep.id
LEFT JOIN finance.chart_of_accounts coa ON b.account_id = coa.id
WHERE b.fiscal_year = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())::INTEGER
GROUP BY b.org_id, b.branch_id, br.name, dep.name, b.fiscal_year,
         b.budget_type, coa.account_name, b.annual_budget;
```

## 🔵 DRUG INTERACTION & FORMULARY

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- PHARMACY: DRUG INTERACTION DATABASE
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE pharmacy.drug_interactions (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    drug_1_generic      VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    drug_2_generic      VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    interaction_type    VARCHAR(50),    -- major/moderate/minor/contraindicated
    mechanism           TEXT,
    clinical_effect     TEXT,
    management          TEXT,
    severity_score      INTEGER,        -- 1-5
    evidence_level      VARCHAR(20),    -- established/probable/suspected/possible
    is_active           BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    reference           TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE pharmacy.formulary_lists (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    name            VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    version         VARCHAR(20),
    effective_date  DATE,
    approved_by     UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'active',
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE pharmacy.formulary_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    formulary_id    UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy.formulary_lists(id),
    medicine_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy.medicines(id),
    category        VARCHAR(50),   -- first_line/second_line/restricted/not_recommended
    restrictions    TEXT,
    alternatives    JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    requires_approval BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

-- Drug interaction check function
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION pharmacy.check_drug_interactions(
    p_patient_id UUID,
    p_new_drug   VARCHAR(300)
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    existing_drug       TEXT,
    interaction_type    TEXT,
    severity            TEXT,
    clinical_effect     TEXT,
    management          TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        mo.drug_name                                    AS existing_drug,
        di.interaction_type,
        di.severity_score::TEXT                        AS severity,
        di.clinical_effect,
        di.management
    FROM clinical.medication_orders mo
    JOIN pharmacy.drug_interactions di ON (
        (LOWER(di.drug_1_generic) = LOWER(p_new_drug)
         AND LOWER(di.drug_2_generic) = LOWER(mo.generic_name))
        OR
        (LOWER(di.drug_2_generic) = LOWER(p_new_drug)
         AND LOWER(di.drug_1_generic) = LOWER(mo.generic_name))
    )
    WHERE mo.patient_id = p_patient_id
      AND mo.status = 'active'
      AND di.is_active = TRUE
    ORDER BY di.severity_score DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- PHARMACY: NARCOTIC DRUG REGISTER
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE pharmacy.narcotic_registers (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    medicine_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy.medicines(id),
    register_date       DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    transaction_type    VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
                        -- receipt/issue/return/wastage/count
    quantity            DECIMAL(10,3) NOT NULL,
    balance             DECIMAL(10,3) NOT NULL,
    patient_id          UUID REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    prescription_no     VARCHAR(50),
    administered_by     UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    witnessed_by        UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    dispensed_by        UUID REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    wastage_amount      DECIMAL(10,3),
    wastage_reason      TEXT,
    batch_no            VARCHAR(100),
    notes               TEXT,
    created_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Validate narcotic balance
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION pharmacy.validate_narcotic_balance()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE v_current_balance DECIMAL;
BEGIN
    SELECT balance INTO v_current_balance
    FROM pharmacy.narcotic_registers
    WHERE branch_id = NEW.branch_id
      AND medicine_id = NEW.medicine_id
    ORDER BY created_at DESC
    LIMIT 1;

    IF NEW.transaction_type IN ('issue', 'wastage') THEN
        IF COALESCE(v_current_balance, 0) < NEW.quantity THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Narcotic balance insufficient. Current: %, Requested: %',
                COALESCE(v_current_balance, 0), NEW.quantity;
        END IF;
        NEW.balance := COALESCE(v_current_balance, 0) - NEW.quantity;
    ELSE
        NEW.balance := COALESCE(v_current_balance, 0) + NEW.quantity;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_narcotic_balance
BEFORE INSERT ON pharmacy.narcotic_registers
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION pharmacy.validate_narcotic_balance();
```

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- CLINICAL: INTER-HOSPITAL TRANSFER
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE clinical.hospital_directory (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    hospital_name   VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    hospital_type   VARCHAR(50),    -- govt/private/specialized/clinic
    address         TEXT,
    city            VARCHAR(100),
    phone           VARCHAR(20),
    email           VARCHAR(150),
    contact_person  VARCHAR(200),
    available_services JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE clinical.inter_hospital_transfers (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    patient_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical.patients(id),
    admission_id        UUID REFERENCES clinical.admissions(id),
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    transfer_no         VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE,

    -- Transfer details
    transfer_type       VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,   -- outgoing/incoming
    transfer_reason     VARCHAR(300),
    transfer_datetime   TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

    -- From
    from_hospital_id    UUID REFERENCES clinical.hospital_directory(id),
    from_hospital_name  VARCHAR(300),
    from_doctor_name    VARCHAR(200),

    -- To
    to_hospital_id      UUID REFERENCES clinical.hospital_directory(id),
    to_hospital_name    VARCHAR(300),
    to_doctor_name      VARCHAR(200),
    to_department       VARCHAR(200),

    -- Patient condition
    condition_at_transfer VARCHAR(50),
    vitals_at_transfer  JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
    iv_access           BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    oxygen_support      BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    ventilator          BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,

    -- Accompanying
    doctor_accompanied  BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    nurse_accompanied   BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    accompanying_staff  VARCHAR(200),
    ambulance_type      VARCHAR(50),

    -- Documents sent
    documents_sent      JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    -- [discharge_summary, lab_reports, xray, prescriptions]

    -- Authorization
    authorized_by       UUID REFERENCES hr.doctors(id),
    patient_consent     BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    guardian_consent    BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,

    status              VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'planned',
    notes               TEXT,
    created_by          UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

## 🔵 TRAINING & DEVELOPMENT MODULE

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- HR: TRAINING & DEVELOPMENT
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE hr.training_programs (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES platform.organizations(id),
    program_name    VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    category        VARCHAR(100),   -- clinical/admin/safety/leadership/technical
    description     TEXT,
    duration_hours  DECIMAL(6,2),
    trainer_name    VARCHAR(200),
    trainer_type    VARCHAR(20),    -- internal/external
    is_mandatory    BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    target_roles    JSONB DEFAULT '[]',  -- which roles must complete
    valid_for_months INTEGER DEFAULT 12, -- certification validity
    max_participants INTEGER,
    cost_per_head   DECIMAL(10,2),
    is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE hr.training_sessions (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    program_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES hr.training_programs(id),
    branch_id       UUID REFERENCES platform.branches(id),
    session_date    DATE NOT NULL,
    start_time      TIME,
    end_time        TIME,
    venue           VARCHAR(200),
    trainer_name    VARCHAR(200),
    max_participants INTEGER,
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'scheduled',
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE hr.training_enrollments (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    session_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES hr.training_sessions(id),
    employee_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    enrolled_at     TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    attended        BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    pre_test_score  DECIMAL(5,2),
    post_test_score DECIMAL(5,2),
    passed          BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    certificate_no  VARCHAR(100),
    certificate_url TEXT,
    valid_until     DATE,
    feedback        TEXT,
    UNIQUE(session_id, employee_id)
);

-- Alert for mandatory training due
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION hr.check_mandatory_training_due()
RETURNS TABLE (
    employee_name   TEXT,
    program_name    TEXT,
    last_completed  DATE,
    valid_until     DATE,
    days_remaining  INTEGER
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        e.full_name                                         AS employee_name,
        tp.program_name,
        MAX(te.session_date)                               AS last_completed,
        MAX(te_enr.valid_until)                            AS valid_until,
        (MAX(te_enr.valid_until) - CURRENT_DATE)::INTEGER  AS days_remaining
    FROM hr.employees e
    CROSS JOIN hr.training_programs tp
    LEFT JOIN hr.training_enrollments te_enr ON te_enr.employee_id = e.id
        AND te_enr.passed = TRUE
    LEFT JOIN hr.training_sessions te ON te_enr.session_id = te.id
        AND te.program_id = tp.id
    WHERE tp.is_mandatory = TRUE
      AND e.status = 'active'
      AND (te_enr.valid_until IS NULL OR te_enr.valid_until <= CURRENT_DATE + 30)
    GROUP BY e.full_name, tp.program_name
    ORDER BY days_remaining ASC NULLS FIRST;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- HR: DISCIPLINARY ACTIONS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE hr.disciplinary_actions (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    employee_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    action_type     VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
                    -- verbal_warning/written_warning/show_cause/
                    -- suspension/termination/demotion
    incident_date   DATE,
    description     TEXT NOT NULL,
    action_date     DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    action_by       UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    employee_response TEXT,
    response_due_date DATE,
    hr_decision     TEXT,
    status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'issued',
    document_url    TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- HR: EMPLOYEE EXIT MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE hr.employee_exits (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    employee_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES hr.employees(id),
    exit_type           VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
                        -- resignation/termination/retirement/contract_end/death
    notice_date         DATE,
    last_working_date   DATE,
    exit_interview_date DATE,
    exit_interview_by   UUID REFERENCES platform.users(id),
    reason              TEXT,
    noc_issued          BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    experience_letter   BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    clearance_done      BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    clearance_details   JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
    -- {finance: true, it: true, hr: true, admin: true}
    final_settlement    DECIMAL(14,2),
    pf_amount           DECIMAL(12,2),
    gratuity_amount     DECIMAL(12,2),
    leave_encashment    DECIMAL(12,2),
    other_dues          DECIMAL(12,2),
    deductions          DECIMAL(12,2),
    net_payable         DECIMAL(14,2),
    paid_at             TIMESTAMPTZ,
    status              VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'initiated',
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Auto-deactivate user on exit
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION hr.deactivate_user_on_exit()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status = 'completed' AND OLD.status != 'completed' THEN
        UPDATE hr.employees
        SET status = 'inactive', end_date = NEW.last_working_date
        WHERE id = NEW.employee_id;

        UPDATE platform.users
        SET is_active = FALSE
        WHERE employee_id = NEW.employee_id;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_employee_exit
AFTER UPDATE ON hr.employee_exits
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION hr.deactivate_user_on_exit();
```

## 🔵 FINAL STORED PROCEDURES (Advanced)

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- SP 36: CANTEEN DAILY SALES REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_canteen_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    canteen_name        TEXT,
    report_date         DATE,
    total_orders        BIGINT,
    total_revenue       NUMERIC,
    cash_revenue        NUMERIC,
    mobile_revenue      NUMERIC,
    avg_order_value     NUMERIC,
    top_item            TEXT,
    staff_orders        BIGINT,
    patient_orders      BIGINT,
    visitor_orders      BIGINT,
    cancelled_orders    BIGINT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        cl.name                                             AS canteen_name,
        co.ordered_at::DATE                                AS report_date,
        COUNT(co.id)::BIGINT                               AS total_orders,
        ROUND(SUM(co.total_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)            AS total_revenue,
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN co.payment_method = 'cash'
               THEN co.total_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS cash_revenue,
        ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN co.payment_method IN ('bkash', 'nagad')
               THEN co.total_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS mobile_revenue,
        ROUND(AVG(co.total_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)            AS avg_order_value,
        (
            SELECT cmi.item_name
            FROM operations.canteen_order_items coi2
            JOIN operations.canteen_orders co2 ON coi2.order_id = co2.id
            JOIN operations.canteen_menu_items cmi ON coi2.menu_item_id = cmi.id
            WHERE co2.canteen_id = cl.id
              AND co2.ordered_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
            GROUP BY cmi.item_name
            ORDER BY SUM(coi2.quantity) DESC
            LIMIT 1
        )                                                   AS top_item,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN co.customer_type = 'staff' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS staff_orders,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN co.customer_type = 'patient' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS patient_orders,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN co.customer_type = 'visitor' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS visitor_orders,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN co.status = 'cancelled' THEN 1 END)::BIGINT AS cancelled_orders
    FROM operations.canteen_locations cl
    LEFT JOIN operations.canteen_orders co ON co.canteen_id = cl.id
        AND co.ordered_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    WHERE cl.branch_id = p_branch_id AND cl.is_active = TRUE
    GROUP BY cl.name, co.ordered_at::DATE
    ORDER BY report_date DESC, cl.name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 37: KITCHEN PRODUCTION REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_kitchen_production_report(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    production_date     DATE,
    meal_type           TEXT,
    recipe_name         TEXT,
    diet_type           TEXT,
    planned_qty         INTEGER,
    actual_qty          INTEGER,
    variance            INTEGER,
    cost_per_meal       NUMERIC,
    total_cost          NUMERIC,
    qc_passed           BOOLEAN
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        kpo.production_date,
        mt.name                                             AS meal_type,
        kr.recipe_name,
        dt.name                                             AS diet_type,
        kpo.planned_quantity                               AS planned_qty,
        COALESCE(kpo.actual_quantity, 0)                   AS actual_qty,
        COALESCE(kpo.actual_quantity, 0) - kpo.planned_quantity AS variance,
        ROUND(COALESCE(kpo.unit_cost, 0)::NUMERIC, 2)      AS cost_per_meal,
        ROUND(COALESCE(kpo.total_cost, 0)::NUMERIC, 2)     AS total_cost,
        COALESCE(kpo.quality_check, FALSE)                 AS qc_passed
    FROM operations.kitchen_production_orders kpo
    JOIN operations.kitchen_locations kl ON kpo.kitchen_id = kl.id
    LEFT JOIN operations.meal_types mt ON kpo.meal_type_id = mt.id
    LEFT JOIN operations.kitchen_recipes kr ON kpo.recipe_id = kr.id
    LEFT JOIN operations.diet_types dt ON kr.diet_type_id = dt.id
    WHERE kl.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND kpo.production_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    ORDER BY kpo.production_date DESC, mt.sort_order;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 38: LINEN MANAGEMENT REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_linen_report(
    p_branch_id UUID
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    linen_category      TEXT,
    total_quantity      INTEGER,
    in_ward             INTEGER,
    in_laundry          INTEGER,
    available           INTEGER,
    condemned           INTEGER,
    utilization_pct     NUMERIC,
    avg_issue_per_day   NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        lc.name                                             AS linen_category,
        li.total_quantity,
        li.in_ward,
        li.in_laundry,
        li.available,
        li.condemned,
        ROUND(
            (li.in_ward + li.in_laundry)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(li.total_quantity, 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                          AS utilization_pct,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(liss.id)::DECIMAL / 30, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                          AS avg_issue_per_day
    FROM operations.linen_inventory li
    JOIN operations.linen_categories lc ON li.category_id = lc.id
    LEFT JOIN operations.linen_issuance liss ON liss.branch_id = li.branch_id
        AND liss.category_id = li.category_id
        AND liss.issued_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days'
    WHERE li.branch_id = p_branch_id
    GROUP BY lc.name, li.total_quantity, li.in_ward,
             li.in_laundry, li.available, li.condemned
    ORDER BY li.total_quantity DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 39: CORPORATE BILLING REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_corporate_billing_report(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    company_name        TEXT,
    total_bills         BIGINT,
    total_employees     BIGINT,
    gross_amount        NUMERIC,
    discount_amount     NUMERIC,
    net_amount          NUMERIC,
    invoiced_amount     NUMERIC,
    paid_amount         NUMERIC,
    outstanding         NUMERIC,
    credit_limit        NUMERIC,
    credit_utilization  NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        cc.company_name,
        COUNT(DISTINCT b.id)::BIGINT                       AS total_bills,
        COUNT(DISTINCT ce.id)::BIGINT                      AS total_employees,
        ROUND(SUM(b.gross_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)             AS gross_amount,
        ROUND(SUM(b.discount_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)          AS discount_amount,
        ROUND(SUM(b.net_amount)::NUMERIC, 2)               AS net_amount,
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(ci.net_amount), 0)::NUMERIC, 2) AS invoiced_amount,
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(ci.paid_amount), 0)::NUMERIC, 2) AS paid_amount,
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(ci.balance_amount), 0)::NUMERIC, 2) AS outstanding,
        cc.credit_limit,
        ROUND(
            COALESCE(SUM(ci.balance_amount), 0) /
            NULLIF(cc.credit_limit, 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                          AS credit_utilization
    FROM finance.corporate_companies cc
    LEFT JOIN finance.corporate_employees ce ON ce.company_id = cc.id AND ce.is_active = TRUE
    LEFT JOIN finance.corporate_invoices ci ON ci.company_id = cc.id
        AND ci.invoice_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    LEFT JOIN finance.corporate_invoice_items cii ON cii.invoice_id = ci.id
    LEFT JOIN finance.bills b ON cii.bill_id = b.id
    WHERE cc.org_id = p_org_id AND cc.is_active = TRUE
    GROUP BY cc.company_name, cc.credit_limit
    ORDER BY net_amount DESC NULLS LAST;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 40: HR TRAINING COMPLIANCE REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_training_compliance(
    p_org_id UUID,
    p_year   INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())::INTEGER
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    program_name        TEXT,
    category            TEXT,
    is_mandatory        BOOLEAN,
    target_employees    BIGINT,
    enrolled            BIGINT,
    completed           BIGINT,
    passed              BIGINT,
    compliance_pct      NUMERIC,
    avg_score           NUMERIC,
    total_hours         NUMERIC,
    total_cost          NUMERIC
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        tp.program_name,
        tp.category,
        tp.is_mandatory,
        COUNT(DISTINCT e.id)::BIGINT                       AS target_employees,
        COUNT(DISTINCT te.employee_id)::BIGINT             AS enrolled,
        COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN te.attended THEN te.employee_id END)::BIGINT AS completed,
        COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN te.passed THEN te.employee_id END)::BIGINT AS passed,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN te.passed THEN te.employee_id END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT e.id), 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                          AS compliance_pct,
        ROUND(AVG(te.post_test_score)::NUMERIC, 1)         AS avg_score,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN te.attended THEN te.employee_id END) *
            tp.duration_hours, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                          AS total_hours,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN te.attended THEN te.employee_id END) *
            COALESCE(tp.cost_per_head, 0), 2
        )::NUMERIC                                          AS total_cost
    FROM hr.training_programs tp
    CROSS JOIN hr.employees e
    LEFT JOIN hr.training_sessions ts ON ts.program_id = tp.id
        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM ts.session_date) = p_year
    LEFT JOIN hr.training_enrollments te ON te.session_id = ts.id
        AND te.employee_id = e.id
    WHERE tp.org_id = p_org_id
      AND tp.is_active = TRUE
      AND e.org_id = p_org_id
      AND e.status = 'active'
    GROUP BY tp.program_name, tp.category, tp.is_mandatory,
             tp.duration_hours, tp.cost_per_head
    ORDER BY tp.is_mandatory DESC, compliance_pct ASC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 41: NARCOTIC DRUG AUDIT REPORT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_narcotic_audit(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    medicine_name       TEXT,
    transaction_date    DATE,
    transaction_type    TEXT,
    quantity            NUMERIC,
    balance             NUMERIC,
    patient_name        TEXT,
    administered_by     TEXT,
    witnessed_by        TEXT,
    dispensed_by        TEXT,
    batch_no            TEXT,
    discrepancy         BOOLEAN
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        m.generic_name || ' ' || m.strength                AS medicine_name,
        nr.register_date                                    AS transaction_date,
        nr.transaction_type,
        nr.quantity,
        nr.balance,
        COALESCE(p.full_name, 'N/A')                       AS patient_name,
        COALESCE(e1.full_name, 'N/A')                      AS administered_by,
        COALESCE(e2.full_name, 'N/A')                      AS witnessed_by,
        COALESCE(e3.full_name, 'N/A')                      AS dispensed_by,
        nr.batch_no,
        -- Discrepancy: balance doesn't match expected
        CASE WHEN ABS(nr.balance - (
            LAG(nr.balance, 1, nr.balance) OVER (
                PARTITION BY nr.medicine_id
                ORDER BY nr.created_at
            ) + CASE WHEN nr.transaction_type = 'receipt' THEN nr.quantity
                     ELSE -nr.quantity END
        )) > 0.001 THEN TRUE ELSE FALSE END                AS discrepancy
    FROM pharmacy.narcotic_registers nr
    JOIN pharmacy.medicines m ON nr.medicine_id = m.id
    LEFT JOIN clinical.patients p ON nr.patient_id = p.id
    LEFT JOIN hr.employees e1 ON nr.administered_by = e1.id
    LEFT JOIN hr.employees e2 ON nr.witnessed_by = e2.id
    LEFT JOIN hr.employees e3 ON nr.dispensed_by = e3.id
    WHERE nr.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND nr.register_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    ORDER BY nr.medicine_id, nr.created_at;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 42: COMPREHENSIVE PATIENT BILL STATEMENT
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_patient_bill_statement(
    p_admission_id UUID
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    service_date        DATE,
    category            TEXT,
    description         TEXT,
    quantity            NUMERIC,
    unit_price          NUMERIC,
    discount            NUMERIC,
    net_amount          NUMERIC,
    covered_by_insurance BOOLEAN
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        COALESCE(bi.service_date, b.bill_date::DATE)       AS service_date,
        bi.item_type                                        AS category,
        bi.item_name                                        AS description,
        bi.quantity,
        ROUND(bi.unit_price::NUMERIC, 2)                   AS unit_price,
        ROUND(bi.discount_amount::NUMERIC, 2)              AS discount,
        ROUND(bi.net_amount::NUMERIC, 2)                   AS net_amount,
        (b.insurance_id IS NOT NULL)                       AS covered_by_insurance
    FROM finance.bills b
    JOIN finance.bill_items bi ON bi.bill_id = b.id
    WHERE b.admission_id = p_admission_id
      AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'draft')
    ORDER BY service_date ASC, bi.item_type, bi.item_name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 43: DRUG UTILIZATION REVIEW (DUR)
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_drug_utilization_review(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    generic_name        TEXT,
    brand_name          TEXT,
    category            TEXT,
    total_prescriptions BIGINT,
    total_quantity      NUMERIC,
    total_value         NUMERIC,
    is_antibiotic       BOOLEAN,
    is_controlled       BOOLEAN,
    generic_usage_pct   NUMERIC,
    top_prescriber      TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        m.generic_name,
        m.brand_name,
        mc.name                                             AS category,
        COUNT(DISTINCT di.id)::BIGINT                      AS total_prescriptions,
        ROUND(SUM(di.quantity)::NUMERIC, 0)                AS total_quantity,
        ROUND(SUM(di.quantity * di.unit_price)::NUMERIC, 2) AS total_value,
        m.is_antibiotic,
        m.is_controlled,
        ROUND(
            COUNT(CASE WHEN pi.is_substitution = FALSE THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(di.id), 0) * 100, 1
        )::NUMERIC                                          AS generic_usage_pct,
        (
            SELECT u.full_name
            FROM pharmacy.prescriptions rx2
            JOIN platform.users u ON rx2.prescribed_by = u.id
            JOIN pharmacy.prescription_items pri2 ON pri2.prescription_id = rx2.id
            WHERE pri2.medicine_id = m.id
              AND rx2.prescribed_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
            GROUP BY u.full_name
            ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC
            LIMIT 1
        )                                                   AS top_prescriber
    FROM pharmacy.dispensing_items di
    JOIN pharmacy.medicines m ON di.medicine_id = m.id
    LEFT JOIN pharmacy.medicine_categories mc ON m.category_id = mc.id
    LEFT JOIN pharmacy.prescription_items pi ON di.rx_item_id = pi.id
    JOIN pharmacy.dispensings d ON di.dispensing_id = d.id
    JOIN platform.branches br ON d.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
      AND d.dispensed_at::DATE BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    GROUP BY m.generic_name, m.brand_name, mc.name,
             m.is_antibiotic, m.is_controlled, m.id
    ORDER BY total_value DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 44: COMPLETE MIS REPORT (Management Info System)
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_mis_report(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_month     INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NOW())::INTEGER,
    p_year      INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())::INTEGER
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    section         TEXT,
    metric          TEXT,
    current_month   NUMERIC,
    previous_month  NUMERIC,
    ytd             NUMERIC,
    target          NUMERIC,
    achievement_pct NUMERIC
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_from_date DATE := TO_DATE(p_year::TEXT || '-' || p_month::TEXT || '-01', 'YYYY-MM-DD');
    v_to_date   DATE := (v_from_date + INTERVAL '1 month - 1 day')::DATE;
    v_prev_from DATE := (v_from_date - INTERVAL '1 month')::DATE;
    v_prev_to   DATE := (v_from_date - INTERVAL '1 day')::DATE;
    v_ytd_from  DATE := TO_DATE(p_year::TEXT || '-01-01', 'YYYY-MM-DD');
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY

    -- CLINICAL SECTION
    SELECT 'Clinical', 'Total IPD Admissions',
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC FROM clinical.admissions a
         JOIN platform.branches br ON a.branch_id = br.id
         WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
           AND a.admission_datetime::DATE BETWEEN v_from_date AND v_to_date),
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC FROM clinical.admissions a
         JOIN platform.branches br ON a.branch_id = br.id
         WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
           AND a.admission_datetime::DATE BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to),
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC FROM clinical.admissions a
         JOIN platform.branches br ON a.branch_id = br.id
         WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
           AND a.admission_datetime::DATE BETWEEN v_ytd_from AND v_to_date),
        NULL::NUMERIC, NULL::NUMERIC

    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Clinical', 'Total OPD Visits',
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC FROM clinical.patient_visits v
         WHERE v.branch_id IN (SELECT id FROM platform.branches WHERE org_id = p_org_id)
           AND v.visit_type = 'opd'
           AND v.visit_date::DATE BETWEEN v_from_date AND v_to_date),
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC FROM clinical.patient_visits v
         WHERE v.branch_id IN (SELECT id FROM platform.branches WHERE org_id = p_org_id)
           AND v.visit_type = 'opd'
           AND v.visit_date::DATE BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to),
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC FROM clinical.patient_visits v
         WHERE v.branch_id IN (SELECT id FROM platform.branches WHERE org_id = p_org_id)
           AND v.visit_type = 'opd'
           AND v.visit_date::DATE BETWEEN v_ytd_from AND v_to_date),
        NULL::NUMERIC, NULL::NUMERIC

    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Clinical', 'Total Surgeries',
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC FROM clinical.surgeries s
         WHERE s.branch_id IN (SELECT id FROM platform.branches WHERE org_id = p_org_id)
           AND s.status = 'completed'
           AND s.actual_start_time::DATE BETWEEN v_from_date AND v_to_date),
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC FROM clinical.surgeries s
         WHERE s.branch_id IN (SELECT id FROM platform.branches WHERE org_id = p_org_id)
           AND s.status = 'completed'
           AND s.actual_start_time::DATE BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to),
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC FROM clinical.surgeries s
         WHERE s.branch_id IN (SELECT id FROM platform.branches WHERE org_id = p_org_id)
           AND s.status = 'completed'
           AND s.actual_start_time::DATE BETWEEN v_ytd_from AND v_to_date),
        NULL::NUMERIC, NULL::NUMERIC

    UNION ALL

    -- FINANCIAL SECTION
    SELECT 'Financial', 'Total Revenue (BDT)',
        (SELECT ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(net_amount), 0)::NUMERIC, 0)
         FROM finance.bills
         WHERE org_id = p_org_id AND status NOT IN ('cancelled','draft')
           AND bill_date::DATE BETWEEN v_from_date AND v_to_date),
        (SELECT ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(net_amount), 0)::NUMERIC, 0)
         FROM finance.bills
         WHERE org_id = p_org_id AND status NOT IN ('cancelled','draft')
           AND bill_date::DATE BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to),
        (SELECT ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(net_amount), 0)::NUMERIC, 0)
         FROM finance.bills
         WHERE org_id = p_org_id AND status NOT IN ('cancelled','draft')
           AND bill_date::DATE BETWEEN v_ytd_from AND v_to_date),
        NULL::NUMERIC,
        NULL::NUMERIC

    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Financial', 'Collection Rate (%)',
        (SELECT ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(paid_amount),0) /
                      NULLIF(SUM(net_amount),0)*100,1)::NUMERIC
         FROM finance.bills WHERE org_id = p_org_id
           AND status NOT IN ('cancelled','draft')
           AND bill_date::DATE BETWEEN v_from_date AND v_to_date),
        (SELECT ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(paid_amount),0) /
                      NULLIF(SUM(net_amount),0)*100,1)::NUMERIC
         FROM finance.bills WHERE org_id = p_org_id
           AND status NOT IN ('cancelled','draft')
           AND bill_date::DATE BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to),
        NULL::NUMERIC, 90.0, NULL::NUMERIC

    UNION ALL

    -- QUALITY SECTION
    SELECT 'Quality', 'Patient Satisfaction Score (/5)',
        (SELECT ROUND(AVG(overall_rating)::NUMERIC, 2)
         FROM operations.patient_feedback pf
         JOIN platform.branches br ON pf.branch_id = br.id
         WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
           AND pf.submitted_at::DATE BETWEEN v_from_date AND v_to_date),
        (SELECT ROUND(AVG(overall_rating)::NUMERIC, 2)
         FROM operations.patient_feedback pf
         JOIN platform.branches br ON pf.branch_id = br.id
         WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
           AND pf.submitted_at::DATE BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to),
        NULL::NUMERIC, 4.0, NULL::NUMERIC

    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Quality', 'Complaints Received',
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC FROM operations.complaints
         WHERE org_id = p_org_id
           AND created_at::DATE BETWEEN v_from_date AND v_to_date),
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC FROM operations.complaints
         WHERE org_id = p_org_id
           AND created_at::DATE BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to),
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::NUMERIC FROM operations.complaints
         WHERE org_id = p_org_id
           AND created_at::DATE BETWEEN v_ytd_from AND v_to_date),
        NULL::NUMERIC, NULL::NUMERIC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

# Hospital ERP - Complete PostgreSQL Schema (Part 6)

## 🔵 COMPLETE ROW LEVEL SECURITY (RLS)


SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY — MULTI-TENANT DATA ISOLATION
-- ============================================================

-- Enable RLS on all major tables
ALTER TABLE clinical.patients           ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE clinical.admissions         ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE clinical.patient_visits     ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE clinical.vital_signs        ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE clinical.lab_orders         ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE clinical.lab_results        ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE clinical.medication_orders  ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE clinical.surgeries          ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE clinical.clinical_notes     ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE finance.bills               ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE finance.payments            ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE finance.insurance_claims    ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE pharmacy.prescriptions      ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE pharmacy.dispensings        ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE pharmacy.medicine_stock     ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE hr.employees               ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE hr.payslips                ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE hr.attendance              ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE operations.complaints       ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE operations.canteen_orders   ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE operations.purchase_orders  ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE facility.beds              ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE facility.wards             ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE facility.equipment_registry ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- ============================================================
-- RLS HELPER FUNCTIONS
-- ============================================================

-- Get current user org_id from session
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION platform.current_org_id()
RETURNS UUID AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN NULLIF(current_setting('app.current_org_id', TRUE), '')::UUID;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN NULL;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER;

-- Get current user branch_id from session
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION platform.current_branch_id()
RETURNS UUID AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN NULLIF(current_setting('app.current_branch_id', TRUE), '')::UUID;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN NULL;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER;

-- Get current user role from session
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION platform.current_user_role()
RETURNS TEXT AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN NULLIF(current_setting('app.current_role', TRUE), '');
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN NULL;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER;

-- Get current user id from session
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION platform.current_app_user_id()
RETURNS UUID AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN NULLIF(current_setting('app.current_user_id', TRUE), '')::UUID;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN NULL;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER;

-- Check if user is super admin
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION platform.is_super_admin()
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN platform.current_user_role() = 'super_admin';
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER;

-- Check if user has branch access
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION platform.has_branch_access(p_branch_id UUID)
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Super admin has all access
    IF platform.is_super_admin() THEN RETURN TRUE; END IF;

    -- Hospital admin has org-level access
    IF platform.current_user_role() = 'hospital_admin' THEN
        RETURN EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM platform.branches
            WHERE id = p_branch_id
              AND org_id = platform.current_org_id()
        );
    END IF;

    -- Others only access their assigned branch or explicitly granted branches
    RETURN p_branch_id = platform.current_branch_id()
        OR EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM platform.user_branch_access
            WHERE user_id = platform.current_app_user_id()
              AND branch_id = p_branch_id
        );
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER;

-- ============================================================
-- RLS POLICIES — CLINICAL.PATIENTS
-- ============================================================

-- Policy: Users can only see patients in their org
CREATE POLICY patients_org_isolation ON clinical.patients
    USING (
        platform.is_super_admin()
        OR org_id = platform.current_org_id()
    );

-- Policy: Doctors only see their own patients (optional - can be enabled)
CREATE POLICY patients_doctor_view ON clinical.patients
    AS PERMISSIVE FOR SELECT
    USING (
        platform.current_user_role() NOT IN ('doctor')
        OR EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM clinical.admissions a
            JOIN hr.doctors d ON a.admitting_doctor_id = d.id
            JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
            WHERE a.patient_id = clinical.patients.id
              AND e.id = (
                  SELECT employee_id FROM platform.users
                  WHERE id = platform.current_app_user_id()
              )
        )
    );

-- ============================================================
-- RLS POLICIES — FINANCE.BILLS
-- ============================================================

CREATE POLICY bills_org_isolation ON finance.bills
    USING (
        platform.is_super_admin()
        OR (
            org_id = platform.current_org_id()
            AND platform.has_branch_access(branch_id)
        )
    );

-- Cashier can only see today's bills
CREATE POLICY bills_cashier_today ON finance.bills
    AS RESTRICTIVE FOR SELECT
    USING (
        platform.current_user_role() != 'cashier'
        OR bill_date::DATE = CURRENT_DATE
    );

-- ============================================================
-- RLS POLICIES — HR.PAYSLIPS
-- ============================================================

-- Employees can only see their own payslips
CREATE POLICY payslips_own_only ON hr.payslips
    USING (
        platform.is_super_admin()
        OR platform.current_user_role() IN ('hospital_admin', 'hr_manager', 'accountant')
        OR employee_id = (
            SELECT e.id FROM hr.employees e
            JOIN platform.users u ON u.employee_id = e.id
            WHERE u.id = platform.current_app_user_id()
        )
    );

-- ============================================================
-- RLS POLICIES — HR.ATTENDANCE
-- ============================================================

CREATE POLICY attendance_own_or_manager ON hr.attendance
    USING (
        platform.is_super_admin()
        OR platform.current_user_role() IN ('hospital_admin', 'hr_manager', 'branch_admin')
        OR employee_id = (
            SELECT e.id FROM hr.employees e
            JOIN platform.users u ON u.employee_id = e.id
            WHERE u.id = platform.current_app_user_id()
        )
    );

-- ============================================================
-- RLS POLICIES — PHARMACY.MEDICINE_STOCK
-- ============================================================

CREATE POLICY stock_branch_isolation ON pharmacy.medicine_stock
    USING (
        platform.is_super_admin()
        OR platform.has_branch_access(branch_id)
    );

-- ============================================================
-- RLS POLICIES — OPERATIONS.PURCHASE_ORDERS
-- ============================================================

CREATE POLICY po_branch_isolation ON operations.purchase_orders
    USING (
        platform.is_super_admin()
        OR platform.has_branch_access(branch_id)
    );

-- Store manager can only see their branch POs
CREATE POLICY po_store_manager ON operations.purchase_orders
    AS RESTRICTIVE FOR SELECT
    USING (
        platform.current_user_role() != 'store_manager'
        OR platform.has_branch_access(branch_id)
    );

-- ============================================================
-- RLS POLICIES — FACILITY TABLES
-- ============================================================

CREATE POLICY wards_branch_isolation ON facility.wards
    USING (
        platform.is_super_admin()
        OR platform.has_branch_access(branch_id)
    );

CREATE POLICY beds_ward_isolation ON facility.beds
    USING (
        platform.is_super_admin()
        OR EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM facility.wards w
            WHERE w.id = facility.beds.ward_id
              AND platform.has_branch_access(w.branch_id)
        )
    );

-- ============================================================
-- APPLICATION SESSION SETUP FUNCTION
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION platform.set_app_session(
    p_user_id   UUID,
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_role      TEXT,
    p_ip        TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS VOID AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Set session variables for RLS
    PERFORM set_config('app.current_user_id',  p_user_id::TEXT,  FALSE);
    PERFORM set_config('app.current_org_id',   p_org_id::TEXT,   FALSE);
    PERFORM set_config('app.current_branch_id',p_branch_id::TEXT,FALSE);
    PERFORM set_config('app.current_role',     p_role,           FALSE);

    IF p_ip IS NOT NULL THEN
        PERFORM set_config('app.client_ip', p_ip, FALSE);
    END IF;

    -- Log session start
    INSERT INTO audit.login_history(
        user_id, username, login_at,
        ip_address, status
    )
    SELECT p_user_id, u.username, NOW(),
           p_ip::INET, 'success'
    FROM platform.users u
    WHERE u.id = p_user_id;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;
```

```
-- ============================================================
-- ROLE-PERMISSION ASSIGNMENTS (KEY ROLES)
-- ============================================================

-- Function to assign permissions to role by slug
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION platform.assign_permissions_to_role(
    p_role_slug TEXT,
    p_permissions TEXT[]  -- array of 'module.action' strings
)
RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
DECLARE
    v_role_id   UUID;
    v_perm      TEXT;
    v_module    TEXT;
    v_action    TEXT;
    v_perm_id   UUID;
    v_count     INTEGER := 0;
BEGIN
    SELECT id INTO v_role_id FROM platform.roles WHERE slug = p_role_slug;
    IF v_role_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE WARNING 'Role not found: %', p_role_slug;
        RETURN 0;
    END IF;

    FOREACH v_perm IN ARRAY p_permissions
    LOOP
        v_module := SPLIT_PART(v_perm, '.', 1) || '.' || SPLIT_PART(v_perm, '.', 2);
        v_action := SPLIT_PART(v_perm, '.', 3);

        SELECT id INTO v_perm_id
        FROM platform.permissions
        WHERE module = v_module AND action = v_action;

        IF v_perm_id IS NOT NULL THEN
            INSERT INTO platform.role_permissions(role_id, permission_id)
            VALUES (v_role_id, v_perm_id)
            ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
            v_count := v_count + 1;
        END IF;
    END LOOP;

    RETURN v_count;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```
