## 🔵 COMPLETE INSURANCE PROCESSING APIs

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- API: INSURANCE PRE-AUTHORIZATION
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION api.submit_preauth_request(
    p_patient_id        UUID,
    p_admission_id      UUID,
    p_policy_id         UUID,
    p_claimed_amount    DECIMAL(14,2),
    p_diagnosis         TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_procedures        JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    p_submitted_by      UUID DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    claim_id        UUID,
    claim_no        VARCHAR,
    policy_no       TEXT,
    claimed_amount  NUMERIC,
    company_name    TEXT,
    status          TEXT,
    message         TEXT
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_claim_id      UUID;
    v_claim_no      VARCHAR(50);
    v_policy        RECORD;
    v_bill_id       UUID;
BEGIN
    -- Get policy details
    SELECT
        pip.policy_no, pip.sum_insured, pip.balance_amount,
        pip.valid_to, ic.name AS company_name
    INTO v_policy
    FROM finance.patient_insurance_policies pip
    JOIN finance.insurance_companies ic ON pip.company_id = ic.id
    WHERE pip.id = p_policy_id;

    -- Validate policy
    IF v_policy.policy_no IS NULL THEN
        RETURN QUERY SELECT
            NULL::UUID, NULL::VARCHAR, NULL::TEXT,
            0::NUMERIC, NULL::TEXT,
            'error'::TEXT, 'Policy not found'::TEXT;
        RETURN;
    END IF;

    IF v_policy.valid_to < CURRENT_DATE THEN
        RETURN QUERY SELECT
            NULL::UUID, NULL::VARCHAR, v_policy.policy_no,
            0::NUMERIC, v_policy.company_name,
            'error'::TEXT, 'Policy expired on ' || v_policy.valid_to::TEXT;
        RETURN;
    END IF;

    IF p_claimed_amount > v_policy.balance_amount THEN
        RETURN QUERY SELECT
            NULL::UUID, NULL::VARCHAR, v_policy.policy_no,
            0::NUMERIC, v_policy.company_name,
            'error'::TEXT,
            'Claimed amount exceeds policy balance: ' || v_policy.balance_amount::TEXT;
        RETURN;
    END IF;

    -- Get admission bill
    SELECT id INTO v_bill_id
    FROM finance.bills
    WHERE admission_id = p_admission_id
      AND status NOT IN ('cancelled')
    ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1;

    -- Create insurance claim
    v_claim_no := 'CLM' || TO_CHAR(NOW(), 'YYYYMMDD') ||
                  LPAD((
                      SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*)+1, 1)::TEXT
                      FROM finance.insurance_claims
                      WHERE claim_date = CURRENT_DATE
                  ), 5, '0');

    INSERT INTO finance.insurance_claims(
        org_id, bill_id, policy_id, patient_id, admission_id,
        claim_no, pre_auth_no, claim_date,
        claimed_amount, status, submitted_at
    )
    SELECT
        br.org_id, v_bill_id, p_policy_id, p_patient_id, p_admission_id,
        v_claim_no, v_claim_no, CURRENT_DATE,
        p_claimed_amount, 'submitted', NOW()
    FROM clinical.admissions a
    JOIN platform.branches br ON a.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE a.id = p_admission_id
    RETURNING id INTO v_claim_id;

    -- Update bill with insurance details
    UPDATE finance.bills
    SET insurance_id = p_policy_id,
        pre_auth_no = v_claim_no
    WHERE id = v_bill_id;

    -- Send notification to insurance team
    INSERT INTO audit.notifications_queue(
        notification_type, reference_id, reference_table,
        message, priority
    ) VALUES (
        'insurance_claim_submitted', v_claim_id, 'finance.insurance_claims',
        'Pre-auth submitted: ' || v_claim_no ||
        ' | Amount: ' || p_claimed_amount ||
        ' | Company: ' || v_policy.company_name,
        'high'
    );

    RETURN QUERY SELECT
        v_claim_id,
        v_claim_no,
        v_policy.policy_no,
        ROUND(p_claimed_amount::NUMERIC, 2),
        v_policy.company_name,
        'success'::TEXT,
        'Pre-authorization request submitted successfully'::TEXT;

EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN QUERY SELECT
        NULL::UUID, NULL::VARCHAR, NULL::TEXT,
        0::NUMERIC, NULL::TEXT,
        'error'::TEXT, SQLERRM::TEXT;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- ============================================================
-- API: SETTLE INSURANCE CLAIM
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION api.settle_insurance_claim(
    p_claim_id          UUID,
    p_approved_amount   DECIMAL(14,2),
    p_settled_amount    DECIMAL(14,2),
    p_settlement_ref    VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL,
    p_settlement_notes  TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_settled_by        UUID DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    claim_no        VARCHAR,
    claimed_amount  NUMERIC,
    approved_amount NUMERIC,
    settled_amount  NUMERIC,
    patient_balance NUMERIC,
    status          TEXT,
    message         TEXT
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_claim         RECORD;
    v_patient_bal   DECIMAL;
BEGIN
    -- Get claim details
    SELECT ic.*, pip.policy_no, b.net_amount AS bill_amount,
           b.paid_amount, b.balance_amount
    INTO v_claim
    FROM finance.insurance_claims ic
    JOIN finance.patient_insurance_policies pip ON ic.policy_id = pip.id
    LEFT JOIN finance.bills b ON ic.bill_id = b.id
    WHERE ic.id = p_claim_id;

    IF v_claim.id IS NULL THEN
        RETURN QUERY SELECT
            NULL::VARCHAR, 0::NUMERIC, 0::NUMERIC, 0::NUMERIC, 0::NUMERIC,
            'error'::TEXT, 'Claim not found'::TEXT;
        RETURN;
    END IF;

    -- Update claim
    UPDATE finance.insurance_claims
    SET
        approved_amount = p_approved_amount,
        settled_amount  = p_settled_amount,
        status          = 'settled',
        settled_at      = NOW(),
        updated_at      = NOW()
    WHERE id = p_claim_id;

    -- Update bill with insurance payment
    UPDATE finance.bills
    SET
        insurance_amount = p_settled_amount,
        balance_amount   = GREATEST(net_amount - paid_amount - p_settled_amount, 0),
        status = CASE
            WHEN net_amount - paid_amount - p_settled_amount <= 0 THEN 'paid'
            ELSE status
        END,
        updated_at = NOW()
    WHERE id = v_claim.bill_id;

    -- Get updated patient balance
    SELECT balance_amount INTO v_patient_bal
    FROM finance.bills WHERE id = v_claim.bill_id;

    -- Reduce policy balance
    UPDATE finance.patient_insurance_policies
    SET balance_amount = GREATEST(balance_amount - p_settled_amount, 0)
    WHERE id = v_claim.policy_id;

    -- Create journal entry for insurance settlement
    INSERT INTO finance.journal_entries(
        org_id, entry_date, entry_type, reference_type,
        reference_id, description, is_posted, posted_at
    )
    SELECT
        br.org_id, CURRENT_DATE, 'receipt', 'insurance_claim',
        p_claim_id,
        'Insurance settlement: ' || v_claim.claim_no ||
        ' - ' || COALESCE(p_settlement_ref, ''),
        TRUE, NOW()
    FROM finance.insurance_claims ic
    JOIN finance.bills b ON ic.bill_id = b.id
    JOIN platform.branches br ON b.branch_id = br.id
    WHERE ic.id = p_claim_id;

    RETURN QUERY SELECT
        v_claim.claim_no,
        ROUND(v_claim.claimed_amount::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(p_approved_amount::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(p_settled_amount::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(v_patient_bal::NUMERIC, 2),
        'success'::TEXT,
        'Insurance claim settled. Patient balance: BDT ' || v_patient_bal::TEXT;

EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN QUERY SELECT
        NULL::VARCHAR, 0::NUMERIC, 0::NUMERIC, 0::NUMERIC, 0::NUMERIC,
        'error'::TEXT, SQLERRM::TEXT;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;
```

## 🔵 COMPLETE OT MANAGEMENT APIs

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- API: SCHEDULE SURGERY
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION api.schedule_surgery(
    p_patient_id        UUID,
    p_admission_id      UUID,
    p_ot_room_id        UUID,
    p_scheduled_dt      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    p_surgery_name      VARCHAR(300),
    p_surgery_type      VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'elective',
    p_primary_surgeon   UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_assistant_surgeon UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_anesthetist       UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_anesthesia_type   VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'general',
    p_icd10_code        VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT NULL,
    p_estimated_hrs     DECIMAL(4,2) DEFAULT 2,
    p_scheduled_by      UUID DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    surgery_id          UUID,
    surgery_slot        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    ot_room_name        TEXT,
    surgeon_name        TEXT,
    conflict_found      BOOLEAN,
    checklist           JSONB,
    status              TEXT,
    message             TEXT
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_surgery_id    UUID;
    v_conflict      BOOLEAN := FALSE;
    v_ot_name       TEXT;
    v_surgeon_name  TEXT;
    v_checklist     JSONB;
    v_branch_id     UUID;
BEGIN
    -- Check OT room availability
    SELECT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM clinical.surgeries s
        WHERE s.ot_room_id = p_ot_room_id
          AND s.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'completed')
          AND tsrange(
              s.scheduled_datetime,
              s.scheduled_datetime + (p_estimated_hrs * INTERVAL '1 hour')
          ) && tsrange(
              p_scheduled_dt,
              p_scheduled_dt + (p_estimated_hrs * INTERVAL '1 hour')
          )
    ) INTO v_conflict;

    -- Get OT room name
    SELECT name INTO v_ot_name
    FROM clinical.ot_rooms WHERE id = p_ot_room_id;

    -- Get surgeon name
    SELECT e.full_name INTO v_surgeon_name
    FROM hr.doctors d
    JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
    WHERE d.id = p_primary_surgeon;

    -- Get branch from admission
    SELECT branch_id INTO v_branch_id
    FROM clinical.admissions WHERE id = p_admission_id;

    -- Build pre-op checklist
    v_checklist := jsonb_build_array(
        jsonb_build_object('task', 'Pre-op assessment',         'done', FALSE, 'required', TRUE),
        jsonb_build_object('task', 'Anesthesia pre-assessment', 'done', FALSE, 'required', TRUE),
        jsonb_build_object('task', 'Fasting status verified',   'done', FALSE, 'required', TRUE),
        jsonb_build_object('task', 'Consent form signed',       'done', FALSE, 'required', TRUE),
        jsonb_build_object('task', 'Allergy check done',        'done', FALSE, 'required', TRUE),
        jsonb_build_object('task', 'Blood availability confirmed','done',FALSE,'required', TRUE),
        jsonb_build_object('task', 'Pre-op investigations done','done', FALSE, 'required', TRUE),
        jsonb_build_object('task', 'IV access established',     'done', FALSE, 'required', FALSE),
        jsonb_build_object('task', 'Patient marking done',      'done', FALSE, 'required', FALSE),
        jsonb_build_object('task', 'OT ready confirmed',        'done', FALSE, 'required', TRUE)
    );

    -- Create surgery record
    INSERT INTO clinical.surgeries(
        branch_id, patient_id, admission_id, ot_room_id,
        scheduled_datetime, surgery_type, surgery_name, icd10_code,
        primary_surgeon_id, assistant_surgeon_id, anesthetist_id,
        anesthesia_type, status, created_by
    ) VALUES (
        v_branch_id, p_patient_id, p_admission_id, p_ot_room_id,
        p_scheduled_dt, p_surgery_type, p_surgery_name, p_icd10_code,
        p_primary_surgeon, p_assistant_surgeon, p_anesthetist,
        p_anesthesia_type, 'scheduled', p_scheduled_by
    ) RETURNING id INTO v_surgery_id;

    -- Add surgery to patient bill
    INSERT INTO finance.bill_items(
        bill_id, item_type, item_name, item_ref_id,
        quantity, unit_price, net_amount, service_date
    )
    SELECT
        b.id, 'ot', 'Surgery: ' || p_surgery_name, v_surgery_id,
        1, 0, 0, p_scheduled_dt::DATE
    FROM finance.bills b
    WHERE b.admission_id = p_admission_id
      AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'paid')
    LIMIT 1;

    -- Notify surgical team
    INSERT INTO audit.notifications_queue(
        notification_type, reference_id, reference_table,
        message, priority
    ) VALUES (
        'surgery_scheduled', v_surgery_id, 'clinical.surgeries',
        'Surgery scheduled: ' || p_surgery_name ||
        ' | OT: ' || v_ot_name ||
        ' | Time: ' || p_scheduled_dt::TEXT ||
        ' | Surgeon: ' || COALESCE(v_surgeon_name, 'TBD'),
        'high'
    );

    RETURN QUERY SELECT
        v_surgery_id,
        p_scheduled_dt,
        v_ot_name,
        v_surgeon_name,
        v_conflict,
        v_checklist,
        CASE WHEN v_conflict THEN 'warning' ELSE 'success' END,
        CASE WHEN v_conflict
             THEN '⚠️ OT time conflict detected! Please review schedule.'
             ELSE 'Surgery scheduled successfully' END;

EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN QUERY SELECT
        NULL::UUID, NULL::TIMESTAMPTZ, NULL::TEXT, NULL::TEXT,
        FALSE, NULL::JSONB, 'error'::TEXT, SQLERRM::TEXT;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- ============================================================
-- API: WHO SURGICAL CHECKLIST COMPLETION
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION api.complete_who_checklist(
    p_surgery_id    UUID,
    p_phase         VARCHAR(20),    -- sign_in/time_out/sign_out
    p_checklist     JSONB,          -- completed checklist items
    p_completed_by  UUID DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    surgery_id      UUID,
    phase           TEXT,
    all_done        BOOLEAN,
    issues_found    JSONB,
    status          TEXT,
    message         TEXT
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_issues    JSONB := '[]'::JSONB;
    v_all_done  BOOLEAN := TRUE;
    v_item      JSONB;
BEGIN
    -- Check for incomplete required items
    FOR v_item IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_array_elements(p_checklist)
    LOOP
        IF (v_item->>'required')::BOOLEAN = TRUE
           AND (v_item->>'done')::BOOLEAN = FALSE THEN
            v_issues := v_issues || v_item;
            v_all_done := FALSE;
        END IF;
    END LOOP;

    -- Update WHO checklist phase
    UPDATE clinical.surgeries
    SET
        who_sign_in_done  = CASE WHEN p_phase = 'sign_in'  THEN TRUE ELSE who_sign_in_done END,
        who_time_out_done = CASE WHEN p_phase = 'time_out' THEN TRUE ELSE who_time_out_done END,
        who_sign_out_done = CASE WHEN p_phase = 'sign_out' THEN TRUE ELSE who_sign_out_done END,
        status = CASE
            WHEN p_phase = 'sign_in'  THEN 'in_progress'
            WHEN p_phase = 'sign_out' THEN 'completed'
            ELSE status
        END,
        actual_start_time = CASE
            WHEN p_phase = 'sign_in' AND actual_start_time IS NULL
            THEN NOW() ELSE actual_start_time END,
        actual_end_time = CASE
            WHEN p_phase = 'sign_out' AND actual_end_time IS NULL
            THEN NOW() ELSE actual_end_time END
    WHERE id = p_surgery_id;

    -- Alert if issues found
    IF NOT v_all_done THEN
        INSERT INTO audit.notifications_queue(
            notification_type, reference_id, reference_table,
            message, priority
        ) VALUES (
            'who_checklist_incomplete', p_surgery_id, 'clinical.surgeries',
            '⚠️ WHO ' || p_phase || ' checklist has ' ||
            jsonb_array_length(v_issues) || ' incomplete required item(s)',
            'urgent'
        );
    END IF;

    RETURN QUERY SELECT
        p_surgery_id,
        p_phase,
        v_all_done,
        v_issues,
        CASE WHEN v_all_done THEN 'success' ELSE 'warning' END,
        CASE WHEN v_all_done
             THEN 'WHO ' || p_phase || ' checklist completed'
             ELSE '⚠️ ' || jsonb_array_length(v_issues) || ' required item(s) incomplete!' END;

EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN QUERY SELECT
        NULL::UUID, NULL::TEXT, FALSE, '[]'::JSONB,
        'error'::TEXT, SQLERRM::TEXT;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;
```

## 🔵 COMPLETE SUPPLY CHAIN APIs

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- API: CREATE PURCHASE REQUISITION WITH APPROVAL
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION api.create_purchase_requisition(
    p_branch_id         UUID,
    p_department_id     UUID,
    p_requested_by      UUID,
    p_required_date     DATE,
    p_priority          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'normal',
    p_items             JSONB,
    -- [{item_id, quantity, unit, estimated_cost, remarks}]
    p_notes             TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    pr_id           UUID,
    pr_no           VARCHAR,
    total_items     INTEGER,
    estimated_cost  NUMERIC,
    status          TEXT,
    message         TEXT
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_pr_id         UUID;
    v_pr_no         VARCHAR(30);
    v_total_cost    DECIMAL := 0;
    v_item          JSONB;
BEGIN
    -- Generate PR number
    v_pr_no := 'PR' || TO_CHAR(NOW(), 'YYYYMM') ||
               LPAD((
                   SELECT COALESCE(COUNT(*)+1,1)::TEXT
                   FROM operations.purchase_requisitions
                   WHERE created_at >= DATE_TRUNC('month', NOW())
               ), 5, '0');

    -- Create PR
    INSERT INTO operations.purchase_requisitions(
        branch_id, pr_no, requested_by, department_id,
        required_date, priority, status, notes
    ) VALUES (
        p_branch_id, v_pr_no, p_requested_by, p_department_id,
        p_required_date, p_priority, 'submitted', p_notes
    ) RETURNING id INTO v_pr_id;

    -- Add PR items
    FOR v_item IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_array_elements(p_items)
    LOOP
        INSERT INTO operations.purchase_requisition_items(
            pr_id, item_id, quantity, unit,
            estimated_cost, remarks
        ) VALUES (
            v_pr_id,
            (v_item->>'item_id')::UUID,
            (v_item->>'quantity')::DECIMAL,
            v_item->>'unit',
            (v_item->>'estimated_cost')::DECIMAL,
            v_item->>'remarks'
        );

        v_total_cost := v_total_cost +
            COALESCE((v_item->>'estimated_cost')::DECIMAL, 0) *
            COALESCE((v_item->>'quantity')::DECIMAL, 0);
    END LOOP;

    -- Notify approver
    INSERT INTO audit.notifications_queue(
        notification_type, reference_id, reference_table,
        message, priority
    ) VALUES (
        'pr_approval_needed', v_pr_id, 'operations.purchase_requisitions',
        'Purchase Requisition ' || v_pr_no ||
        ' awaiting approval. Est. cost: BDT ' || v_total_cost::TEXT,
        CASE WHEN p_priority = 'critical' THEN 'urgent'
             WHEN p_priority = 'high' THEN 'high' ELSE 'normal' END
    );

    RETURN QUERY SELECT
        v_pr_id,
        v_pr_no,
        jsonb_array_length(p_items),
        ROUND(v_total_cost::NUMERIC, 2),
        'success'::TEXT,
        'Purchase requisition ' || v_pr_no || ' submitted for approval'::TEXT;

EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN QUERY SELECT
        NULL::UUID, NULL::VARCHAR, 0, 0::NUMERIC,
        'error'::TEXT, SQLERRM::TEXT;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- ============================================================
-- API: POST GRN (Goods Receipt Note)
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION api.post_grn(
    p_grn_id        UUID,
    p_qc_status     VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'passed',
    p_qc_by         UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_qc_notes      TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    grn_no          VARCHAR,
    total_items     BIGINT,
    total_value     NUMERIC,
    stock_updated   BOOLEAN,
    status          TEXT,
    message         TEXT
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_grn       RECORD;
    v_total_val DECIMAL := 0;
BEGIN
    -- Get GRN
    SELECT g.*, po.branch_id AS br_id
    INTO v_grn
    FROM operations.goods_receipt_notes g
    JOIN operations.purchase_orders po ON g.po_id = po.id
    WHERE g.id = p_grn_id AND g.status = 'draft';

    IF v_grn.id IS NULL THEN
        RETURN QUERY SELECT
            NULL::VARCHAR, 0::BIGINT, 0::NUMERIC, FALSE,
            'error'::TEXT, 'GRN not found or already posted'::TEXT;
        RETURN;
    END IF;

    -- Calculate total value
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(accepted_qty * unit_price), 0)
    INTO v_total_val
    FROM operations.grn_items
    WHERE grn_id = p_grn_id;

    -- Update QC status and post
    UPDATE operations.goods_receipt_notes
    SET
        qc_status  = p_qc_status,
        qc_by      = p_qc_by,
        status     = 'posted',
        posted_at  = NOW(),
        notes      = COALESCE(p_qc_notes, notes)
    WHERE id = p_grn_id;

    -- Update PO received quantities
    UPDATE operations.purchase_order_items poi
    SET received_qty = poi.received_qty + gi.accepted_qty
    FROM operations.grn_items gi
    WHERE gi.po_item_id = poi.id
      AND gi.grn_id = p_grn_id;

    -- Update PO status
    UPDATE operations.purchase_orders po
    SET status = CASE
        WHEN NOT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM operations.purchase_order_items
            WHERE po_id = po.id AND pending_qty > 0
        ) THEN 'received'
        ELSE 'partial'
    END
    WHERE id = v_grn.po_id;

    -- Notify store manager
    INSERT INTO audit.notifications_queue(
        notification_type, reference_id, reference_table,
        message, priority
    ) VALUES (
        'grn_posted', p_grn_id, 'operations.goods_receipt_notes',
        'GRN ' || v_grn.grn_no || ' posted. Stock updated. Value: BDT ' || v_total_val::TEXT,
        'normal'
    );

    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        v_grn.grn_no,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM operations.grn_items WHERE grn_id = p_grn_id),
        ROUND(v_total_val::NUMERIC, 2),
        TRUE,
        'success'::TEXT,
        'GRN posted and stock updated successfully'::TEXT;

EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN QUERY SELECT
        NULL::VARCHAR, 0::BIGINT, 0::NUMERIC, FALSE,
        'error'::TEXT, SQLERRM::TEXT;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;
```

## 🔵 COMPLETE HR PAYROLL API

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- API: PROCESS MONTHLY PAYROLL (Complete)
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION api.run_payroll(
    p_org_id        UUID,
    p_branch_id     UUID,
    p_month         INTEGER,
    p_year          INTEGER,
    p_processed_by  UUID
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    run_id              UUID,
    total_employees     BIGINT,
    total_gross         NUMERIC,
    total_deductions    NUMERIC,
    total_net           NUMERIC,
    total_tax           NUMERIC,
    errors_count        INTEGER,
    status              TEXT,
    message             TEXT
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_run_id        UUID;
    v_working_days  INTEGER := 26;
    v_errors        INTEGER := 0;
    v_total_gross   DECIMAL := 0;
    v_total_ded     DECIMAL := 0;
    v_total_net     DECIMAL := 0;
    v_total_tax     DECIMAL := 0;
    v_emp           RECORD;
    v_present_days  DECIMAL;
    v_leave_days    DECIMAL;
    v_ot_hours      DECIMAL;
    v_gross         DECIMAL;
    v_deduction     DECIMAL;
    v_tax           DECIMAL;
    v_net           DECIMAL;
    v_advance_ded   DECIMAL;
BEGIN
    -- Create payroll run
    INSERT INTO hr.payroll_runs(
        org_id, branch_id, pay_month, pay_year, status, processed_at
    ) VALUES (
        p_org_id, p_branch_id, p_month, p_year, 'processing', NOW()
    )
    ON CONFLICT (org_id, branch_id, pay_month, pay_year)
    DO UPDATE SET status = 'processing', processed_at = NOW()
    RETURNING id INTO v_run_id;

    -- Process each employee
    FOR v_emp IN
        SELECT e.id, e.basic_salary, e.full_name, e.employee_code
        FROM hr.employees e
        WHERE e.org_id = p_org_id
          AND e.branch_id = p_branch_id
          AND e.status = 'active'
    LOOP
        BEGIN
            -- Get attendance
            SELECT
                COUNT(CASE WHEN status = 'present' THEN 1 END),
                COUNT(CASE WHEN status = 'late' THEN 1 END) * 0.5,
                COALESCE(SUM(overtime_hours), 0)
            INTO v_present_days, v_leave_days, v_ot_hours
            FROM hr.attendance
            WHERE employee_id = v_emp.id
              AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM attendance_date) = p_month
              AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM attendance_date) = p_year;

            -- Get approved leaves
            SELECT COALESCE(SUM(days), 0) INTO v_leave_days
            FROM hr.leave_applications
            WHERE employee_id = v_emp.id
              AND status = 'approved'
              AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM from_date) = p_month
              AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM from_date) = p_year;

            -- Calculate salary components
            -- Basic
            v_gross := v_emp.basic_salary;
            -- House rent (40% of basic)
            v_gross := v_gross + (v_emp.basic_salary * 0.40);
            -- Medical (10% of basic)
            v_gross := v_gross + (v_emp.basic_salary * 0.10);
            -- Transport allowance
            v_gross := v_gross + 1500;

            -- Overtime
            v_gross := v_gross +
                (v_ot_hours * (v_emp.basic_salary / v_working_days / 8) * 2);

            -- Attendance deduction for absences
            v_gross := v_gross -
                (GREATEST(v_working_days - COALESCE(v_present_days, 0) -
                          COALESCE(v_leave_days, 0), 0) *
                 (v_emp.basic_salary / v_working_days));

            -- Calculate deductions
            -- Provident fund (10% of basic)
            v_deduction := v_emp.basic_salary * 0.10;

            -- Income tax (simplified slab)
            v_tax := CASE
                WHEN v_gross * 12 <= 350000 THEN 0
                WHEN v_gross * 12 <= 450000 THEN (v_gross * 12 - 350000) * 0.05 / 12
                WHEN v_gross * 12 <= 750000 THEN 5000/12 + (v_gross * 12 - 450000) * 0.10 / 12
                ELSE 35000/12 + (v_gross * 12 - 750000) * 0.15 / 12
            END;
            v_deduction := v_deduction + v_tax;

            -- Net salary
            v_net := v_gross - v_deduction;

            -- Upsert payslip
            INSERT INTO hr.payslips(
                payroll_run_id, employee_id, pay_month, pay_year,
                working_days, present_days, leave_days, overtime_hours,
                basic_salary, gross_salary, total_allowance,
                total_deduction, income_tax, provident_fund,
                net_salary, status
            ) VALUES (
                v_run_id, v_emp.id, p_month, p_year,
                v_working_days, COALESCE(v_present_days, 0),
                COALESCE(v_leave_days, 0), COALESCE(v_ot_hours, 0),
                v_emp.basic_salary, v_gross,
                v_gross - v_emp.basic_salary,
                v_deduction, v_tax, v_emp.basic_salary * 0.10,
                v_net, 'draft'
            )
            ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;

            v_total_gross := v_total_gross + v_gross;
            v_total_ded   := v_total_ded + v_deduction;
            v_total_net   := v_total_net + v_net;
            v_total_tax   := v_total_tax + v_tax;

        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
            v_errors := v_errors + 1;
        END;
    END LOOP;

    -- Update payroll run totals
    UPDATE hr.payroll_runs
    SET
        status          = 'completed',
        total_gross     = v_total_gross,
        total_deduction = v_total_ded,
        total_net       = v_total_net,
        approved_by     = p_processed_by
    WHERE id = v_run_id;

    RETURN QUERY SELECT
        v_run_id,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM hr.payslips WHERE payroll_run_id = v_run_id)::BIGINT,
        ROUND(v_total_gross::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(v_total_ded::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(v_total_net::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(v_total_tax::NUMERIC, 2),
        v_errors,
        CASE WHEN v_errors > 0 THEN 'partial' ELSE 'success' END,
        'Payroll processed: ' || v_total_gross::TEXT ||
        ' gross. Errors: ' || v_errors::TEXT;

EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN QUERY SELECT
        NULL::UUID, 0::BIGINT, 0::NUMERIC, 0::NUMERIC,
        0::NUMERIC, 0::NUMERIC, 0::INTEGER,
        'error'::TEXT, SQLERRM::TEXT;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;
```

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- MASTER DATA SEEDING
```

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- COMPLETE SCHEMA OBJECT COUNT QUERY
-- ============================================================

SELECT
    'TABLES'        AS object_type,
    COUNT(*)        AS count
FROM information_schema.tables
WHERE table_schema IN (
    'platform','clinical','facility','pharmacy',
    'finance','hr','operations','audit','analytics','api'
)
AND table_type = 'BASE TABLE'

UNION ALL SELECT 'VIEWS',         COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.views
WHERE table_schema IN (
    'platform','clinical','facility','pharmacy',
    'finance','hr','operations','audit','analytics','api'
)

UNION ALL SELECT 'FUNCTIONS',     COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.routines
WHERE routine_schema IN (
    'platform','clinical','facility','pharmacy',
    'finance','hr','operations','audit','analytics','api'
)

UNION ALL SELECT 'TRIGGERS',      COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.triggers
WHERE trigger_schema IN (
    'platform','clinical','facility','pharmacy',
    'finance','hr','operations','audit','analytics'
)

UNION ALL SELECT 'SEQUENCES',     COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.sequences
WHERE sequence_schema IN (
    'platform','clinical','facility','pharmacy',
    'finance','hr','operations','audit','analytics'
);
```

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- OPERATIONAL VIEWS: REAL-TIME DASHBOARDS
-- ============================================================

-- 1. Real-Time Patient Flow Dashboard
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_patient_flow_realtime AS
WITH hourly_stats AS (
    SELECT
        DATE_TRUNC('hour', visit_date)          AS hour_block,
        branch_id,
        visit_type,
        COUNT(*)                                AS visits,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN status = 'active' THEN 1 END) AS active
    FROM clinical.patient_visits
    WHERE visit_date >= NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'
    GROUP BY DATE_TRUNC('hour', visit_date), branch_id, visit_type
)
SELECT
    hs.branch_id,
    br.name                                     AS branch_name,
    hs.hour_block,
    hs.visit_type,
    hs.visits,
    hs.active,
    -- Rolling 7-day average
    ROUND(AVG(hs.visits) OVER (
        PARTITION BY hs.branch_id, hs.visit_type
        ORDER BY hs.hour_block
        ROWS BETWEEN 167 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW
    )::NUMERIC, 1)                              AS rolling_avg_7d
FROM hourly_stats hs
JOIN platform.branches br ON hs.branch_id = br.id
ORDER BY hs.hour_block DESC;

-- ============================================================
-- 2. Ward Census Real-Time View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_ward_census_realtime AS
SELECT
    w.branch_id,
    br.name                                     AS branch_name,
    w.id                                        AS ward_id,
    w.name                                      AS ward_name,
    w.ward_type,
    COUNT(b.id)                                 AS total_beds,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied'    THEN 1 END) AS occupied,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'available'   THEN 1 END) AS available,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'cleaning'    THEN 1 END) AS cleaning,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'maintenance' THEN 1 END) AS maintenance,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'reserved'    THEN 1 END) AS reserved,
    ROUND(
        COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
        NULLIF(COUNT(b.id), 0) * 100, 1
    )                                           AS occupancy_pct,
    -- Admissions today
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.admission_datetime::DATE = CURRENT_DATE THEN 1 END) AS admissions_today,
    -- Expected discharges today
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.expected_discharge_date = CURRENT_DATE
               AND a.status = 'admitted' THEN 1 END) AS expected_discharges,
    -- Long stay patients (>14 days)
    COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied'
               AND EXTRACT(DAY FROM NOW() - a.admission_datetime) > 14
               THEN 1 END)                      AS long_stay_patients,
    -- Average LOS of current patients
    ROUND(AVG(
        CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied'
        THEN EXTRACT(DAY FROM NOW() - a.admission_datetime) END
    )::NUMERIC, 1)                              AS avg_current_los
FROM facility.wards w
JOIN platform.branches br ON w.branch_id = br.id
LEFT JOIN facility.beds b ON b.ward_id = w.id AND b.is_active = TRUE
LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON b.current_admission_id = a.id
WHERE w.is_active = TRUE
GROUP BY w.branch_id, br.name, w.id, w.name, w.ward_type
ORDER BY w.ward_type, w.name;

-- ============================================================
-- 3. Pharmacy Stock Alert View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_pharmacy_alerts AS
SELECT
    ms.branch_id,
    br.name                                     AS branch_name,
    m.id                                        AS medicine_id,
    m.generic_name,
    m.brand_name,
    m.form,
    m.strength,
    m.is_controlled,
    m.is_narcotic,
    SUM(ms.quantity)                            AS total_qty,
    MIN(ms.expiry_date)                         AS nearest_expiry,
    SUM(CASE WHEN ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE
             THEN ms.quantity ELSE 0 END)        AS expired_qty,
    SUM(CASE WHEN ms.expiry_date BETWEEN CURRENT_DATE
                  AND CURRENT_DATE + 30
             THEN ms.quantity ELSE 0 END)        AS expiring_30d,
    SUM(CASE WHEN ms.expiry_date BETWEEN CURRENT_DATE + 31
                  AND CURRENT_DATE + 90
             THEN ms.quantity ELSE 0 END)        AS expiring_90d,
    -- Average daily consumption (last 30 days)
    ROUND(
        COALESCE((
            SELECT SUM(di.quantity)::DECIMAL / 30
            FROM pharmacy.dispensing_items di
            JOIN pharmacy.dispensings d ON di.dispensing_id = d.id
            WHERE di.medicine_id = m.id
              AND d.branch_id = ms.branch_id
              AND d.dispensed_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days'
        ), 0), 1
    )                                           AS avg_daily_consumption,
    -- Days of stock remaining
    ROUND(
        SUM(ms.quantity)::DECIMAL /
        NULLIF(
            COALESCE((
                SELECT SUM(di.quantity)::DECIMAL / 30
                FROM pharmacy.dispensing_items di
                JOIN pharmacy.dispensings d ON di.dispensing_id = d.id
                WHERE di.medicine_id = m.id
                  AND d.branch_id = ms.branch_id
                  AND d.dispensed_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days'
            ), 0.01), 0
        ), 0
    )                                           AS days_stock_remaining,
    -- Alert level
    CASE
        WHEN SUM(ms.quantity) = 0                THEN '🔴 OUT OF STOCK'
        WHEN MIN(ms.expiry_date) < CURRENT_DATE  THEN '🔴 EXPIRED'
        WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN ms.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE
                 THEN ms.quantity ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN '🔴 HAS EXPIRED STOCK'
        WHEN MIN(ms.expiry_date) <= CURRENT_DATE + 30 THEN '🟠 EXPIRING SOON'
        WHEN ROUND(SUM(ms.quantity)::DECIMAL /
             NULLIF(COALESCE((
                 SELECT SUM(di2.quantity)::DECIMAL/30
                 FROM pharmacy.dispensing_items di2
                 JOIN pharmacy.dispensings d2 ON di2.dispensing_id=d2.id
                 WHERE di2.medicine_id=m.id AND d2.branch_id=ms.branch_id
                   AND d2.dispensed_at >= NOW()-INTERVAL '30 days'
             ),0.01),0), 0) < 7                 THEN '🟡 LOW STOCK (<7 days)'
        ELSE                                         '🟢 ADEQUATE'
    END                                         AS alert_level
FROM pharmacy.medicine_stock ms
JOIN pharmacy.medicines m ON ms.medicine_id = m.id
JOIN platform.branches br ON ms.branch_id = br.id
WHERE ms.status = 'active'
GROUP BY ms.branch_id, br.name, m.id, m.generic_name, m.brand_name,
         m.form, m.strength, m.is_controlled, m.is_narcotic
HAVING SUM(ms.quantity) = 0
    OR MIN(ms.expiry_date) <= CURRENT_DATE + 90
    OR ROUND(SUM(ms.quantity)::DECIMAL /
       NULLIF(COALESCE((
           SELECT SUM(di3.quantity)::DECIMAL/30
           FROM pharmacy.dispensing_items di3
           JOIN pharmacy.dispensings d3 ON di3.dispensing_id=d3.id
           WHERE di3.medicine_id=m.id AND d3.branch_id=ms.branch_id
             AND d3.dispensed_at >= NOW()-INTERVAL '30 days'
       ),0.01),0), 0) < 14;

-- ============================================================
-- 4. Doctor Schedule Today View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_doctor_schedule_today AS
SELECT
    d.id                                        AS doctor_id,
    e.full_name                                 AS doctor_name,
    d.specialization,
    dep.name                                    AS department,
    e.branch_id,
    br.name                                     AS branch_name,
    -- OPD Schedule
    ops.session                                 AS opd_session,
    ops.start_time                              AS opd_start,
    ops.end_time                                AS opd_end,
    ops.room_no,
    ops.max_patients,
    -- Today's appointments
    COUNT(a.id)                                 AS booked_today,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'completed' THEN 1 END) AS completed,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'arrived' THEN 1 END)   AS waiting,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'no_show' THEN 1 END)   AS no_show,
    -- Next appointment
    (SELECT p.full_name FROM clinical.appointments ap
     JOIN clinical.patients p ON ap.patient_id = p.id
     WHERE ap.doctor_id = d.id
       AND ap.appointment_datetime::DATE = CURRENT_DATE
       AND ap.status IN ('booked','arrived')
     ORDER BY ap.appointment_datetime LIMIT 1) AS next_patient,
    (SELECT ap.token_no FROM clinical.appointments ap
     WHERE ap.doctor_id = d.id
       AND ap.appointment_datetime::DATE = CURRENT_DATE
       AND ap.status IN ('booked','arrived')
     ORDER BY ap.appointment_datetime LIMIT 1) AS next_token,
    -- On leave today
    EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM hr.doctor_leaves dl
        WHERE dl.doctor_id = d.id
          AND CURRENT_DATE BETWEEN dl.from_date AND dl.to_date
          AND dl.status = 'approved'
    )                                           AS on_leave_today,
    -- IPD patients under care
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM clinical.admissions adm
     WHERE adm.admitting_doctor_id = d.id
       AND adm.status = 'admitted')             AS current_ipd_patients
FROM hr.doctors d
JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
JOIN hr.departments dep ON e.department_id = dep.id
JOIN platform.branches br ON e.branch_id = br.id
LEFT JOIN hr.doctor_opd_schedules ops ON ops.doctor_id = d.id
    AND ops.day_of_week = EXTRACT(DOW FROM CURRENT_DATE)::INTEGER
    AND ops.is_active = TRUE
LEFT JOIN clinical.appointments a ON a.doctor_id = d.id
    AND a.appointment_datetime::DATE = CURRENT_DATE
WHERE d.is_active = TRUE
GROUP BY d.id, e.full_name, d.specialization, dep.name,
         e.branch_id, br.name, ops.session, ops.start_time,
         ops.end_time, ops.room_no, ops.max_patients;

-- ============================================================
-- 5. Financial Collection Real-Time View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_collection_realtime AS
SELECT
    b.branch_id,
    br.name                                         AS branch_name,
    pay.payment_date::DATE                          AS collection_date,
    pay.payment_method,
    COUNT(pay.id)                                   AS transactions,
    ROUND(SUM(pay.amount)::NUMERIC, 2)              AS total_amount,
    -- By hour
    EXTRACT(HOUR FROM pay.payment_date)             AS hour_of_day,
    -- Running total today
    ROUND(SUM(pay.amount) OVER (
        PARTITION BY b.branch_id, pay.payment_date::DATE
        ORDER BY pay.payment_date
        ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW
    )::NUMERIC, 2)                                  AS running_total
FROM finance.payments pay
JOIN finance.bills b ON pay.bill_id = b.id
JOIN platform.branches br ON b.branch_id = br.id
WHERE pay.payment_date >= CURRENT_DATE
  AND pay.status = 'success'
GROUP BY b.branch_id, br.name, pay.payment_date::DATE,
         pay.payment_method, pay.payment_date,
         EXTRACT(HOUR FROM pay.payment_date)
ORDER BY pay.payment_date DESC;

-- ============================================================
-- 6. Pending Tasks Dashboard View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_pending_tasks_dashboard AS
SELECT
    br.id                                       AS branch_id,
    br.name                                     AS branch_name,
    'Pending Lab Results'                       AS task_category,
    COUNT(*)                                    AS task_count,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN lo.priority = 'stat' THEN 1 END) AS urgent_count,
    MIN(lo.ordered_at)                          AS oldest_task
FROM clinical.lab_orders lo
JOIN platform.branches br ON lo.branch_id = br.id
WHERE lo.status IN ('pending', 'collected', 'processing')
GROUP BY br.id, br.name

UNION ALL

SELECT
    br.id, br.name,
    'Unsigned Clinical Notes',
    COUNT(*),
    0, MIN(cn.note_date)
FROM clinical.clinical_notes cn
JOIN clinical.admissions a ON cn.admission_id = a.id
JOIN platform.branches br ON a.branch_id = br.id
WHERE cn.is_final = FALSE AND cn.note_date >= CURRENT_DATE - 2
GROUP BY br.id, br.name

UNION ALL

SELECT
    br.id, br.name,
    'Unverified Lab Results',
    COUNT(*),
    COUNT(CASE WHEN lr.is_critical THEN 1 END),
    MIN(lr.entered_at)
FROM clinical.lab_results lr
JOIN clinical.lab_order_items loi ON lr.order_item_id = loi.id
JOIN clinical.lab_orders lo ON loi.order_id = lo.id
JOIN platform.branches br ON lo.branch_id = br.id
WHERE lr.status = 'draft'
GROUP BY br.id, br.name

UNION ALL

SELECT
    br.id, br.name,
    'Pending Discharge Clearance',
    COUNT(*),
    0, MIN(a.expected_discharge_date::TIMESTAMPTZ)
FROM clinical.admissions a
JOIN platform.branches br ON a.branch_id = br.id
WHERE a.expected_discharge_date <= CURRENT_DATE
  AND a.status = 'admitted'
GROUP BY br.id, br.name

UNION ALL

SELECT
    br.id, br.name,
    'Beds Awaiting Cleaning',
    COUNT(*),
    COUNT(CASE WHEN bct.assigned_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '2 hours' THEN 1 END),
    MIN(bct.assigned_at)
FROM facility.bed_cleaning_tasks bct
JOIN facility.beds b ON bct.bed_id = b.id
JOIN facility.wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
JOIN platform.branches br ON w.branch_id = br.id
WHERE bct.status = 'pending'
GROUP BY br.id, br.name

UNION ALL

SELECT
    br.id, br.name,
    'Unapproved Purchase Requisitions',
    COUNT(*),
    COUNT(CASE WHEN pr.priority IN ('critical','high') THEN 1 END),
    MIN(pr.created_at)
FROM operations.purchase_requisitions pr
JOIN platform.branches br ON pr.branch_id = br.id
WHERE pr.status = 'submitted'
GROUP BY br.id, br.name

ORDER BY branch_name, urgent_count DESC, task_count DESC;

-- ============================================================
-- 7. Staff On Duty View (Real-Time)
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_staff_on_duty AS
SELECT
    ns.ward_id,
    w.name                                      AS ward_name,
    w.branch_id,
    br.name                                     AS branch_name,
    ns.shift_type,
    ns.shift_date,
    ns.start_time,
    ns.end_time,
    e.id                                        AS employee_id,
    e.full_name                                 AS nurse_name,
    des.name                                    AS designation,
    -- Patients assigned (from ward)
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM clinical.admissions a
     WHERE a.ward_id = ns.ward_id AND a.status = 'admitted') AS ward_patient_count,
    -- Medications due in next 2 hours
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM clinical.medication_orders mo
     JOIN clinical.admissions a ON mo.admission_id = a.id
     WHERE a.ward_id = ns.ward_id
       AND mo.status = 'active'
       AND NOT EXISTS (
           SELECT 1 FROM clinical.medication_administrations ma
           WHERE ma.order_id = mo.id
             AND ma.administered_at > NOW() - INTERVAL '6 hours'
       )) AS pending_meds,
    -- Vitals due (not recorded in last 4 hours)
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM clinical.admissions a
     WHERE a.ward_id = ns.ward_id AND a.status = 'admitted'
       AND NOT EXISTS (
           SELECT 1 FROM clinical.vital_signs vs
           WHERE vs.patient_id = a.patient_id
             AND vs.recorded_at > NOW() - INTERVAL '4 hours'
       )) AS vitals_overdue,
    ns.status
FROM clinical.nurse_shifts ns
JOIN hr.employees e ON ns.nurse_id = e.id
JOIN hr.designations des ON e.designation_id = des.id
JOIN facility.wards w ON ns.ward_id = w.id
JOIN platform.branches br ON w.branch_id = br.id
WHERE ns.shift_date = CURRENT_DATE
  AND ns.status = 'scheduled'
ORDER BY w.name, ns.shift_type;

-- ============================================================
-- 8. Equipment Maintenance Due View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_maintenance_due AS
SELECT
    er.branch_id,
    br.name                                     AS branch_name,
    er.equipment_name,
    er.equipment_code,
    er.serial_no,
    er.category,
    er.status                                   AS equipment_status,
    w.name                                      AS location,
    ms.schedule_type,
    ms.task_description,
    ms.next_due_date,
    CURRENT_DATE - ms.next_due_date             AS days_overdue,
    ms.estimated_hours,
    ms.estimated_cost,
    er.warranty_expiry,
    er.amc_end_date,
    er.next_calibration,
    -- Alert level
    CASE
        WHEN ms.next_due_date < CURRENT_DATE - 7  THEN '🔴 Critical Overdue'
        WHEN ms.next_due_date < CURRENT_DATE       THEN '🟠 Overdue'
        WHEN ms.next_due_date <= CURRENT_DATE + 7  THEN '🟡 Due This Week'
        ELSE                                            '🟢 Upcoming'
    END                                         AS maintenance_alert,
    -- Warranty alert
    CASE
        WHEN er.warranty_expiry < CURRENT_DATE           THEN '❌ Expired'
        WHEN er.warranty_expiry <= CURRENT_DATE + 30     THEN '⚠️ Expiring Soon'
        ELSE                                                  '✅ Active'
    END                                         AS warranty_status
FROM facility.equipment_registry er
JOIN platform.branches br ON er.branch_id = br.id
LEFT JOIN facility.wards w ON er.location_ward_id = w.id
JOIN facility.maintenance_schedules ms ON ms.equipment_id = er.id
    AND ms.is_active = TRUE
    AND ms.next_due_date <= CURRENT_DATE + 30
WHERE er.status = 'active'
ORDER BY ms.next_due_date ASC, er.equipment_name;

-- ============================================================
-- 9. Insurance Claims Pipeline View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_insurance_pipeline AS
SELECT
    ic.org_id,
    ins.name                                    AS insurance_company,
    ic.claim_no,
    p.full_name                                 AS patient_name,
    p.mrn,
    a.admission_datetime::DATE                  AS admission_date,
    ic.claim_date,
    ic.claimed_amount,
    COALESCE(ic.approved_amount, 0)             AS approved_amount,
    COALESCE(ic.settled_amount, 0)              AS settled_amount,
    ic.claimed_amount - COALESCE(ic.settled_amount, 0) AS outstanding,
    ic.status,
    ic.submitted_at,
    -- Days in current status
    CURRENT_DATE - COALESCE(ic.submitted_at::DATE, ic.claim_date) AS days_pending,
    -- Status flags
    CASE
        WHEN ic.status = 'rejected'                 THEN '🔴 Rejected'
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE - ic.submitted_at::DATE > 90 THEN '🔴 Aged >90 Days'
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE - ic.submitted_at::DATE > 60 THEN '🟠 Aged >60 Days'
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE - ic.submitted_at::DATE > 30 THEN '🟡 Aged >30 Days'
        WHEN ic.status = 'query'                    THEN '🟡 Query Raised'
        WHEN ic.status = 'approved'                 THEN '🟢 Approved'
        WHEN ic.status = 'submitted'                THEN '🔵 Submitted'
        ELSE                                             '⚪ ' || ic.status
    END                                         AS pipeline_status,
    ic.rejection_reason
FROM finance.insurance_claims ic
JOIN finance.patient_insurance_policies pip ON ic.policy_id = pip.id
JOIN finance.insurance_companies ins ON pip.company_id = ins.id
JOIN clinical.patients p ON ic.patient_id = p.id
LEFT JOIN clinical.admissions a ON ic.admission_id = a.id
WHERE ic.status NOT IN ('settled')
ORDER BY days_pending DESC, ic.claimed_amount DESC;

-- ============================================================
-- 10. HR Attendance Summary Today
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_attendance_today AS
SELECT
    e.branch_id,
    br.name                                     AS branch_name,
    dep.name                                    AS department,
    COUNT(e.id)                                 AS total_staff,
    COUNT(att.id)                               AS marked_attendance,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN att.status = 'present' THEN 1 END) AS present,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN att.status = 'absent'  THEN 1 END) AS absent,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN att.status = 'late'    THEN 1 END) AS late,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN att.status = 'leave'   THEN 1 END) AS on_leave,
    COUNT(e.id) - COUNT(att.id)                 AS not_marked,
    ROUND(
        COUNT(CASE WHEN att.status = 'present' THEN 1 END)::DECIMAL /
        NULLIF(COUNT(e.id), 0) * 100, 1
    )                                           AS attendance_pct,
    -- Staff on duty now (checked in, not checked out)
    COUNT(CASE WHEN att.check_in IS NOT NULL
               AND att.check_out IS NULL THEN 1 END) AS currently_on_duty
FROM hr.employees e
JOIN platform.branches br ON e.branch_id = br.id
JOIN hr.departments dep ON e.department_id = dep.id
LEFT JOIN hr.attendance att ON att.employee_id = e.id
    AND att.attendance_date = CURRENT_DATE
WHERE e.status = 'active'
GROUP BY e.branch_id, br.name, dep.name
ORDER BY br.name, dep.name;

-- ============================================================
-- 11. Blood Bank Status View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_blood_bank_status AS
SELECT
    bbi.branch_id,
    br.name                                     AS branch_name,
    bbi.blood_group,
    bbi.component,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'available' THEN 1 END) AS available_units,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'reserved'  THEN 1 END) AS reserved_units,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'expired'   THEN 1 END) AS expired_units,
    -- Expiring in 7 days
    COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'available'
               AND bbi.expiry_date BETWEEN CURRENT_DATE
                   AND CURRENT_DATE + 7 THEN 1 END)      AS expiring_7d,
    -- Pending requests
    COUNT(DISTINCT br2.id)                               AS pending_requests,
    -- Last received
    MAX(bbi.collection_date)                            AS last_received,
    -- Alert
    CASE
        WHEN COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'available' THEN 1 END) = 0 THEN '🔴 OUT'
        WHEN COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'available' THEN 1 END) <= 2 THEN '🟠 CRITICAL LOW'
        WHEN COUNT(CASE WHEN bbi.status = 'available' THEN 1 END) <= 5 THEN '🟡 LOW'
        ELSE '🟢 ADEQUATE'
    END                                                 AS stock_alert
FROM operations.blood_bank_inventory bbi
JOIN platform.branches br ON bbi.branch_id = br.id
LEFT JOIN operations.blood_requests br2 ON br2.branch_id = bbi.branch_id
    AND br2.blood_group = bbi.blood_group
    AND br2.component = bbi.component
    AND br2.status = 'pending'
GROUP BY bbi.branch_id, br.name, bbi.blood_group, bbi.component
ORDER BY bbi.blood_group, bbi.component;

-- ============================================================
-- 12. Canteen Revenue View
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW analytics.v_canteen_revenue AS
SELECT
    co.canteen_id,
    cl.name                                     AS canteen_name,
    cl.branch_id,
    br.name                                     AS branch_name,
    co.ordered_at::DATE                         AS sale_date,
    COUNT(co.id)                                AS total_orders,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN co.status = 'cancelled' THEN 1 END) AS cancelled,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN co.customer_type = 'staff'   THEN 1 END) AS staff_orders,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN co.customer_type = 'patient' THEN 1 END) AS patient_orders,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN co.customer_type = 'visitor' THEN 1 END) AS visitor_orders,
    ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN co.status != 'cancelled'
               THEN co.total_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS gross_revenue,
    ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN co.status != 'cancelled'
               THEN co.discount_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS discount,
    ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN co.status != 'cancelled'
               THEN co.total_amount - co.discount_amount
               ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 2)         AS net_revenue,
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN co.status != 'cancelled'
               THEN co.total_amount END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS avg_order_value,
    -- By payment method
    ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN co.payment_method = 'cash' AND co.status != 'cancelled'
               THEN co.total_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS cash_revenue,
    ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN co.payment_method IN ('bkash','nagad')
                       AND co.status != 'cancelled'
               THEN co.total_amount ELSE 0 END)::NUMERIC, 2) AS mobile_revenue
FROM operations.canteen_orders co
JOIN operations.canteen_locations cl ON co.canteen_id = cl.id
JOIN platform.branches br ON cl.branch_id = br.id
WHERE co.ordered_at >= CURRENT_DATE - 30
GROUP BY co.canteen_id, cl.name, cl.branch_id, br.name, co.ordered_at::DATE
ORDER BY sale_date DESC, net_revenue DESC;
```

---

## 🔵 COMPLETE STORED PROCEDURES (Final Set)

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- SP 48: COMPLETE MONTH-END CLOSING PROCEDURE
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_month_end_closing(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_month     INTEGER,
    p_year      INTEGER,
    p_closed_by UUID
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    step_no     INTEGER,
    step_name   TEXT,
    status      TEXT,
    records     BIGINT,
    message     TEXT
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_period_start  DATE;
    v_period_end    DATE;
    v_count         BIGINT;
BEGIN
    v_period_start := TO_DATE(p_year||'-'||p_month||'-01','YYYY-MM-DD');
    v_period_end   := (v_period_start + INTERVAL '1 month - 1 day')::DATE;

    -- Step 1: Verify all bills finalized
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count
    FROM finance.bills
    WHERE org_id = p_org_id
      AND bill_date::DATE BETWEEN v_period_start AND v_period_end
      AND status = 'draft';

    RETURN QUERY SELECT 1, 'Draft Bills Check'::TEXT,
        CASE WHEN v_count = 0 THEN '✅ Pass' ELSE '⚠️ Warning' END,
        v_count,
        CASE WHEN v_count = 0 THEN 'All bills finalized'
             ELSE v_count||' draft bills found - please finalize' END;

    -- Step 2: Verify payroll processed
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count
    FROM hr.employees e
    WHERE e.org_id = p_org_id AND e.status = 'active'
      AND NOT EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM hr.payslips ps
          JOIN hr.payroll_runs pr ON ps.payroll_run_id = pr.id
          WHERE ps.employee_id = e.id
            AND pr.pay_month = p_month
            AND pr.pay_year = p_year
            AND pr.status IN ('approved','paid')
      );

    RETURN QUERY SELECT 2, 'Payroll Completion Check'::TEXT,
        CASE WHEN v_count = 0 THEN '✅ Pass' ELSE '🔴 Fail' END,
        v_count,
        CASE WHEN v_count = 0 THEN 'Payroll complete for all employees'
             ELSE v_count||' employees missing payroll' END;

    -- Step 3: Check unposted journal entries
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count
    FROM finance.journal_entries
    WHERE org_id = p_org_id
      AND entry_date BETWEEN v_period_start AND v_period_end
      AND is_posted = FALSE;

    RETURN QUERY SELECT 3, 'Unposted Journal Entries'::TEXT,
        CASE WHEN v_count = 0 THEN '✅ Pass' ELSE '⚠️ Warning' END,
        v_count,
        CASE WHEN v_count = 0 THEN 'All journal entries posted'
             ELSE v_count||' unposted entries found' END;

    -- Step 4: Verify bank reconciliation
    RETURN QUERY SELECT 4, 'Bank Reconciliation'::TEXT,
        '⚠️ Manual'::TEXT, 0::BIGINT,
        'Please complete bank reconciliation manually'::TEXT;

    -- Step 5: Check pending insurance claims
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count
    FROM finance.insurance_claims ic
    WHERE ic.org_id = p_org_id
      AND ic.claim_date BETWEEN v_period_start AND v_period_end
      AND ic.status NOT IN ('settled','rejected');

    RETURN QUERY SELECT 5, 'Pending Insurance Claims'::TEXT,
        CASE WHEN v_count = 0 THEN '✅ Pass' ELSE '🟡 Pending' END,
        v_count,
        v_count||' claims still pending settlement'::TEXT;

    -- Step 6: Refresh materialized views
    PERFORM analytics.refresh_all_mv();

    RETURN QUERY SELECT 6, 'Materialized Views Refresh'::TEXT,
        '✅ Done'::TEXT, 0::BIGINT,
        'All materialized views refreshed'::TEXT;

    -- Step 7: Generate period summary
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count
    FROM finance.bills
    WHERE org_id = p_org_id
      AND bill_date::DATE BETWEEN v_period_start AND v_period_end
      AND status NOT IN ('cancelled','draft');

    RETURN QUERY SELECT 7, 'Period Summary'::TEXT,
        '✅ Complete'::TEXT,
        v_count,
        'Month-end closing completed for ' ||
        TO_CHAR(v_period_start, 'Month YYYY')||
        '. Total bills: '||v_count::TEXT;

    -- Log closing action
    INSERT INTO audit.audit_logs(
        user_id, action, schema_name, table_name,
        record_id, new_values, created_at
    ) VALUES (
        p_closed_by, 'MONTH_END_CLOSE', 'finance', 'period_closing',
        p_org_id::TEXT,
        jsonb_build_object(
            'month', p_month, 'year', p_year,
            'closed_at', NOW(), 'closed_by', p_closed_by
        ),
        NOW()
    );
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 49: COMPLETE PATIENT STATEMENT (360° View)
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_patient_360_view(
    p_patient_id UUID
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    section         TEXT,
    item_date       DATE,
    category        TEXT,
    summary         TEXT,
    amount          NUMERIC,
    status          TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY

    -- Demographics
    SELECT 'PROFILE'::TEXT, NULL::DATE, 'Personal Info'::TEXT,
        p.full_name||' | MRN: '||p.mrn||
        ' | DOB: '||COALESCE(p.date_of_birth::TEXT,'N/A')||
        ' | Gender: '||COALESCE(p.gender,'N/A')||
        ' | Blood: '||COALESCE(p.blood_group,'N/A')||
        ' | Phone: '||COALESCE(p.phone,'N/A'),
        NULL::NUMERIC, p.patient_type
    FROM clinical.patients p WHERE p.id = p_patient_id

    UNION ALL

    -- Allergies
    SELECT 'ALLERGIES', NULL, pa.allergen_type,
        pa.allergen_name||' - Reaction: '||COALESCE(pa.reaction,'N/A')||
        ' | Severity: '||COALESCE(pa.severity,'N/A'),
        NULL, pa.status::TEXT
    FROM clinical.patient_allergies pa
    WHERE pa.patient_id = p_patient_id AND pa.status = 'active'

    UNION ALL

    -- Chronic conditions
    SELECT 'MEDICAL HISTORY', pmh.onset_date, pmh.history_type,
        pmh.condition||COALESCE(' - '||pmh.notes,''),
        NULL, CASE WHEN pmh.is_active THEN 'Active' ELSE 'Resolved' END
    FROM clinical.patient_medical_history pmh
    WHERE pmh.patient_id = p_patient_id
    ORDER BY pmh.onset_date DESC NULLS LAST

    UNION ALL

    -- OPD Visits
    SELECT 'VISITS', v.visit_date::DATE, v.visit_type,
        'Visit #'||COALESCE(v.visit_no,'N/A')||
        ' | Complaint: '||COALESCE(v.chief_complaint,'N/A'),
        NULL, v.status
    FROM clinical.patient_visits v
    WHERE v.patient_id = p_patient_id
    ORDER BY v.visit_date DESC

    UNION ALL

    -- IPD Admissions
    SELECT 'ADMISSIONS', a.admission_datetime::DATE, 'IPD',
        'Admission: '||a.admission_no||
        ' | Ward: '||COALESCE(w.name,'N/A')||
        ' | LOS: '||COALESCE(a.length_of_stay::TEXT,'Current')||' days'||
        ' | Dx: '||COALESCE(a.primary_diagnosis,'N/A'),
        NULL, a.status
    FROM clinical.admissions a
    LEFT JOIN facility.wards w ON a.ward_id = w.id
    WHERE a.patient_id = p_patient_id
    ORDER BY a.admission_datetime DESC

    UNION ALL

    -- Surgeries
    SELECT 'SURGERIES', s.scheduled_datetime::DATE, 'Surgery',
        COALESCE(s.surgery_name,'N/A')||
        ' | OT: '||COALESCE(ot.name,'N/A')||
        ' | Duration: '||COALESCE(s.duration_minutes::TEXT,'N/A')||' min',
        NULL, s.status
    FROM clinical.surgeries s
    LEFT JOIN clinical.ot_rooms ot ON s.ot_room_id = ot.id
    WHERE s.patient_id = p_patient_id
    ORDER BY s.scheduled_datetime DESC

    UNION ALL

    -- Bills & Payments
    SELECT 'BILLING', b.bill_date::DATE, b.bill_type,
        'Bill: '||b.bill_no||
        ' | Net: BDT '||ROUND(b.net_amount,2)::TEXT||
        ' | Paid: BDT '||ROUND(b.paid_amount,2)::TEXT||
        ' | Balance: BDT '||ROUND(b.balance_amount,2)::TEXT,
        ROUND(b.net_amount::NUMERIC,2), b.status
    FROM finance.bills b
    WHERE b.patient_id = p_patient_id
      AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled','draft')
    ORDER BY b.bill_date DESC

    UNION ALL

    -- Prescriptions
    SELECT 'PRESCRIPTIONS', rx.prescribed_at::DATE, rx.prescription_type,
        'Rx: '||rx.rx_no||' | '||
        (SELECT STRING_AGG(pi.drug_name, ', ') FROM pharmacy.prescription_items pi
         WHERE pi.prescription_id = rx.id LIMIT 5),
        NULL, rx.status
    FROM pharmacy.prescriptions rx
    WHERE rx.patient_id = p_patient_id
    ORDER BY rx.prescribed_at DESC

    ORDER BY item_date DESC NULLS LAST, section;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 50: COMPLETE STOCK REORDER REPORT
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_reorder_report(
    p_org_id UUID
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    branch_name         TEXT,
    store_name          TEXT,
    item_code           TEXT,
    item_name           TEXT,
    category            TEXT,
    current_qty         NUMERIC,
    reorder_level       INTEGER,
    min_stock           INTEGER,
    max_stock           INTEGER,
    avg_daily_usage     NUMERIC,
    days_remaining      NUMERIC,
    suggested_qty       NUMERIC,
    last_supplier       TEXT,
    last_purchase_price NUMERIC,
    estimated_cost      NUMERIC,
    urgency             TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    WITH stock_data AS (
        SELECT
            cs.store_id,
            cs.item_id,
            cs.current_qty,
            cs.reorder_level,
            cs.min_stock,
            im.max_stock,
            -- Average daily usage (last 30 days)
            COALESCE((
                SELECT SUM(out_qty)::DECIMAL / 30
                FROM operations.stock_ledger sl2
                WHERE sl2.store_id = cs.store_id
                  AND sl2.item_id = cs.item_id
                  AND sl2.transaction_type IN ('issue','transfer_out')
                  AND sl2.transaction_date >= CURRENT_DATE - 30
            ), 0) AS avg_daily_usage
        FROM operations.mv_current_stock cs
        JOIN operations.item_master im ON cs.item_id = im.id
        WHERE cs.current_qty <= im.reorder_level
    )
    SELECT
        br.name,
        st.name,
        im.item_code,
        im.item_name,
        ic.name,
        ROUND(sd.current_qty::NUMERIC, 2),
        sd.reorder_level,
        sd.min_stock,
        COALESCE(sd.max_stock, 0),
        ROUND(sd.avg_daily_usage::NUMERIC, 2),
        ROUND(CASE WHEN sd.avg_daily_usage > 0
                   THEN sd.current_qty / sd.avg_daily_usage
                   ELSE 999 END::NUMERIC, 1),
        -- Suggested order quantity
        ROUND(GREATEST(
            COALESCE(sd.max_stock, sd.reorder_level * 3) - sd.current_qty,
            sd.reorder_level * 2
        )::NUMERIC, 0),
        -- Last supplier
        (SELECT s.name FROM finance.suppliers s
         JOIN operations.purchase_orders po ON po.supplier_id = s.id
         JOIN operations.purchase_order_items poi ON poi.po_id = po.id
         WHERE poi.item_id = sd.item_id
         ORDER BY po.po_date DESC LIMIT 1),
        -- Last purchase price
        ROUND((
            SELECT poi.unit_price FROM operations.purchase_order_items poi
            JOIN operations.purchase_orders po ON poi.po_id = po.id
            WHERE poi.item_id = sd.item_id
            ORDER BY po.po_date DESC LIMIT 1
        )::NUMERIC, 2),
        -- Estimated cost
        ROUND((
            GREATEST(
                COALESCE(sd.max_stock, sd.reorder_level * 3) - sd.current_qty,
                sd.reorder_level * 2
            ) *
            COALESCE((
                SELECT poi.unit_price FROM operations.purchase_order_items poi
                JOIN operations.purchase_orders po ON poi.po_id = po.id
                WHERE poi.item_id = sd.item_id
                ORDER BY po.po_date DESC LIMIT 1
            ), 0)
        )::NUMERIC, 2),
        CASE
            WHEN sd.current_qty = 0           THEN '🔴 OUT OF STOCK'
            WHEN sd.current_qty <= sd.min_stock THEN '🔴 CRITICAL'
            WHEN sd.avg_daily_usage > 0
             AND sd.current_qty/sd.avg_daily_usage < 3 THEN '🟠 URGENT'
            ELSE                                         '🟡 REORDER'
        END
    FROM stock_data sd
    JOIN operations.stores st ON sd.store_id = st.id
    JOIN platform.branches br ON st.branch_id = br.id
    JOIN operations.item_master im ON sd.item_id = im.id
    JOIN operations.item_categories ic ON im.category_id = ic.id
    WHERE br.org_id = p_org_id
    ORDER BY
        CASE WHEN sd.current_qty = 0 THEN 0
             WHEN sd.current_qty <= sd.min_stock THEN 1
             ELSE 2 END,
        sd.current_qty ASC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 51: COMPLETE DUTY ROSTER REPORT
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_duty_roster(
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_to_date   DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE + 6,
    p_ward_id   UUID DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    employee_name   TEXT,
    designation     TEXT,
    department      TEXT,
    shift_date      DATE,
    shift_type      TEXT,
    ward_name       TEXT,
    start_time      TIME,
    end_time        TIME,
    status          TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    -- Nurse Shifts
    SELECT
        e.full_name,
        des.name,
        dep.name,
        ns.shift_date,
        ns.shift_type,
        w.name,
        ns.start_time,
        ns.end_time,
        ns.status
    FROM clinical.nurse_shifts ns
    JOIN hr.employees e ON ns.nurse_id = e.id
    JOIN hr.designations des ON e.designation_id = des.id
    JOIN hr.departments dep ON e.department_id = dep.id
    JOIN facility.wards w ON ns.ward_id = w.id
    WHERE w.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND ns.shift_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
      AND (p_ward_id IS NULL OR ns.ward_id = p_ward_id)

    UNION ALL

    -- Doctor Duties
    SELECT
        e.full_name,
        des.name,
        dep.name,
        gen_date::DATE,
        ops.session,
        COALESCE(ops.room_no, 'OPD'),
        ops.start_time,
        ops.end_time,
        'scheduled'
    FROM hr.doctor_opd_schedules ops
    JOIN hr.doctors d ON ops.doctor_id = d.id
    JOIN hr.employees e ON d.employee_id = e.id
    JOIN hr.designations des ON e.designation_id = des.id
    JOIN hr.departments dep ON e.department_id = dep.id
    CROSS JOIN generate_series(p_from_date, p_to_date, '1 day'::INTERVAL) AS gen_date
    WHERE ops.branch_id = p_branch_id
      AND ops.is_active = TRUE
      AND ops.day_of_week = EXTRACT(DOW FROM gen_date)::INTEGER
      AND NOT EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM hr.doctor_leaves dl
          WHERE dl.doctor_id = ops.doctor_id
            AND gen_date::DATE BETWEEN dl.from_date AND dl.to_date
            AND dl.status = 'approved'
      )

    ORDER BY shift_date, start_time, employee_name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE;

-- ============================================================
-- SP 52: COST CENTER ANALYSIS
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION analytics.sp_cost_center_analysis(
    p_org_id    UUID,
    p_year      INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(YEAR FROM NOW())::INTEGER,
    p_month     INTEGER DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    department_name     TEXT,
    branch_name         TEXT,
    -- Revenue
    total_revenue       NUMERIC,
    -- Costs
    salary_cost         NUMERIC,
    medicine_cost       NUMERIC,
    maintenance_cost    NUMERIC,
    supply_cost         NUMERIC,
    other_cost          NUMERIC,
    total_cost          NUMERIC,
    -- Profitability
    gross_profit        NUMERIC,
    profit_margin_pct   NUMERIC,
    -- Per patient
    revenue_per_patient NUMERIC,
    cost_per_patient    NUMERIC,
    -- Patient volume
    total_patients      BIGINT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        dep.name                                            AS department_name,
        br.name                                             AS branch_name,
        -- Revenue from bills
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(DISTINCT bi.net_amount), 0)::NUMERIC, 2) AS total_revenue,
        -- Salary cost
        ROUND(COALESCE((
            SELECT SUM(ps.gross_salary)
            FROM hr.payslips ps
            JOIN hr.payroll_runs pr ON ps.payroll_run_id = pr.id
            JOIN hr.employees emp ON ps.employee_id = emp.id
            WHERE emp.department_id = dep.id
              AND pr.pay_year = p_year
              AND (p_month IS NULL OR pr.pay_month = p_month)
        ), 0)::NUMERIC, 2)                                  AS salary_cost,
        -- Medicine cost (from dispensing)
        ROUND(COALESCE((
            SELECT SUM(di.quantity * di.unit_price)
            FROM pharmacy.dispensing_items di
            JOIN pharmacy.dispensings d ON di.dispensing_id = d.id
            JOIN platform.branches br2 ON d.branch_id = br2.id
            WHERE br2.id = br.id
              AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM d.dispensed_at) = p_year
              AND (p_month IS NULL OR EXTRACT(MONTH FROM d.dispensed_at) = p_month)
        ), 0)::NUMERIC, 2)                                  AS medicine_cost,
        -- Maintenance cost
        ROUND(COALESCE((
            SELECT SUM(wo.total_cost)
            FROM facility.maintenance_work_orders wo
            JOIN facility.equipment_registry er ON wo.equipment_id = er.id
            WHERE er.branch_id = br.id
              AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM wo.completed_at) = p_year
              AND (p_month IS NULL OR EXTRACT(MONTH FROM wo.completed_at) = p_month)
        ), 0)::NUMERIC, 2)                                  AS maintenance_cost,
        -- Supply cost
        ROUND(COALESCE((
            SELECT SUM(sl.out_qty * sl.unit_cost)
            FROM operations.stock_ledger sl
            JOIN operations.stores st ON sl.store_id = st.id
            WHERE st.branch_id = br.id
              AND sl.transaction_type = 'issue'
              AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM sl.transaction_date) = p_year
              AND (p_month IS NULL OR EXTRACT(MONTH FROM sl.transaction_date) = p_month)
        ), 0)::NUMERIC, 2)                                  AS supply_cost,
        -- Other costs (placeholder)
        0::NUMERIC                                          AS other_cost,
        -- Total cost
        ROUND((
            COALESCE((SELECT SUM(ps.gross_salary) FROM hr.payslips ps
                      JOIN hr.payroll_runs pr ON ps.payroll_run_id = pr.id
                      JOIN hr.employees emp ON ps.employee_id = emp.id
                      WHERE emp.department_id = dep.id AND pr.pay_year = p_year
                        AND (p_month IS NULL OR pr.pay_month = p_month)), 0) +
            COALESCE((SELECT SUM(wo.total_cost) FROM facility.maintenance_work_orders wo
                      JOIN facility.equipment_registry er ON wo.equipment_id = er.id
                      WHERE er.branch_id = br.id
                        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM wo.completed_at) = p_year), 0)
        )::NUMERIC, 2)                                      AS total_cost,
        -- Gross profit
        ROUND((
            COALESCE(SUM(DISTINCT bi.net_amount), 0) -
            COALESCE((SELECT SUM(ps.gross_salary) FROM hr.payslips ps
                      JOIN hr.payroll_runs pr ON ps.payroll_run_id = pr.id
                      JOIN hr.employees emp ON ps.employee_id = emp.id
                      WHERE emp.department_id = dep.id AND pr.pay_year = p_year), 0)
        )::NUMERIC, 2)                                      AS gross_profit,
        -- Profit margin
        ROUND(
            (COALESCE(SUM(DISTINCT bi.net_amount), 0) -
             COALESCE((SELECT SUM(ps.gross_salary) FROM hr.payslips ps
                       JOIN hr.payroll_runs pr ON ps.payroll_run_id = pr.id
                       JOIN hr.employees emp ON ps.employee_id = emp.id
                       WHERE emp.department_id = dep.id AND pr.pay_year = p_year), 0)
            ) / NULLIF(COALESCE(SUM(DISTINCT bi.net_amount), 0), 0) * 100
        ::NUMERIC, 1)                                       AS profit_margin_pct,
        -- Revenue per patient
        ROUND(
            COALESCE(SUM(DISTINCT bi.net_amount), 0) /
            NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT b.patient_id), 0)
        ::NUMERIC, 2)                                       AS revenue_per_patient,
        0::NUMERIC                                          AS cost_per_patient,
        COUNT(DISTINCT b.patient_id)::BIGINT                AS total_patients
    FROM hr.departments dep
    JOIN platform.branches br ON br.org_id = p_org_id
    LEFT JOIN finance.bills b ON b.branch_id = br.id
        AND b.status NOT IN ('cancelled','draft')
        AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM b.bill_date) = p_year
        AND (p_month IS NULL OR EXTRACT(MONTH FROM b.bill_date) = p_month)
    LEFT JOIN finance.bill_items bi ON bi.bill_id = b.id
    WHERE dep.org_id = p_org_id
    GROUP BY dep.name, br.name, dep.id, br.id
    ORDER BY total_revenue DESC NULLS LAST;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE;
```

## 📊 COMPLETE DATABASE OBJECT VERIFICATION

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- FINAL DATABASE STATISTICS VIEW
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW analytics.v_database_summary AS
WITH object_counts AS (
    SELECT
        'Tables'        AS type, COUNT(*) AS cnt
    FROM information_schema.tables
    WHERE table_schema IN (
        'platform','clinical','facility','pharmacy',
        'finance','hr','operations','audit','analytics','api'
    ) AND table_type = 'BASE TABLE'

    UNION ALL SELECT 'Views', COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.views
    WHERE table_schema IN (
        'platform','clinical','facility','pharmacy',
        'finance','hr','operations','audit','analytics','api'
    )

    UNION ALL SELECT 'Stored Procedures/Functions', COUNT(*)
    FROM information_schema.routines
    WHERE routine_schema IN (
        'platform','clinical','facility','pharmacy',
        'finance','hr','operations','audit','analytics','api'
    )

    UNION ALL SELECT 'Triggers', COUNT(*)
    FROM information_schema.triggers
    WHERE trigger_schema IN (
        'platform','clinical','facility','pharmacy',
        'finance','hr','operations','audit','analytics'
    )

    UNION ALL SELECT 'Sequences', COUNT(*)
    FROM information_schema.sequences
    WHERE sequence_schema IN (
        'platform','clinical','facility','pharmacy',
        'finance','hr','operations','audit','analytics'
    )

    UNION ALL SELECT 'Indexes', COUNT(*)
    FROM pg_indexes
    WHERE schemaname IN (
        'platform','clinical','facility','pharmacy',
        'finance','hr','operations','audit','analytics'
    )

    UNION ALL SELECT 'RLS Policies', COUNT(*)
    FROM pg_policies
    WHERE schemaname IN (
        'platform','clinical','facility','pharmacy',
        'finance','hr','operations','audit','analytics'
    )
)
SELECT
    type                AS object_type,
    cnt                 AS object_count,
    NOW()               AS checked_at
FROM object_counts
ORDER BY cnt DESC;

-- ============================================================
-- GENERATE COMPLETE SCHEMA DOCUMENTATION
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION audit.generate_schema_docs(
    p_schema_name TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    schema_name     TEXT,
    table_name      TEXT,
    column_name     TEXT,
    data_type       TEXT,
    is_nullable     TEXT,
    column_default  TEXT,
    description     TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT
        c.table_schema::TEXT,
        c.table_name::TEXT,
        c.column_name::TEXT,
        c.data_type::TEXT,
        c.is_nullable::TEXT,
        c.column_default::TEXT,
        COALESCE(
            obj_description(
                (c.table_schema||'.'||c.table_name)::REGCLASS,
                'pg_class'
            )::TEXT,
            ''
        )
    FROM information_schema.columns c
    WHERE c.table_schema IN (
        'platform','clinical','facility','pharmacy',
        'finance','hr','operations','audit','analytics','api'
    )
    AND (p_schema_name IS NULL OR c.table_schema = p_schema_name)
    ORDER BY c.table_schema, c.table_name, c.ordinal_position;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE;
```
